# Georgia market rollout (Peach1234 / DLKG)

JVL's core land-based market — the conventional **Georgia co-op / COAM** business — and the software releases (**Peach1234** and **DLKG**) needed to keep it and roll out the new payment-kiosk/printer support. Worked through on the Feb 19 2025 outstanding-subjects alignment call with [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]], [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina]] (re-attributed per owner ruling 2026-07-18: QA/engineering → Shishko) and [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan]]. [[raw/Alignment_outstanding_subjects_2025-02-19.md]]

## Why it matters (Val's revenue warning)

- Val expects a **~40% drop in JVL's conventional Georgia revenue** because two main competitors entered last year. If the main market's revenue falls and the new markets aren't rolled out, JVL "cannot pay wages" / sustain the company. He distinguished **rollout priority** (getting the product into the *marketplace* in Georgia, South Dakota, and Puerto Rico) from development priority — GLI and the Eclipse lab "don't care about timing," so a release manager must drive market rollout. [[raw/Alignment_outstanding_subjects_2025-02-19.md]]

## Dual SAS + third dedicated printer port

- Georgia previously required **dual SAS** (two independent SAS ports on two DB9 connectors, primary + secondary, with the primary controlled by the Georgia Lottery Corporation / "GLC") — implemented last year. [[raw/Alignment_outstanding_subjects_2025-02-19.md]]
- Now that pay-kiosks (relying on the TITO model) need an on-board printer, JVL is adding a **third dedicated printer port**: a new DB9 connector (a **harness**, not a "jumper") inside the CPU box, driven by a **hardware-abstraction-layer library** (Pavel corrected Val that "firmware" is wrong — the printer's firmware sits on the printer; JVL's side is a HAL library). Two printer library types: **Gen 2 / Gen 5 (JCM) + nano-optics** share one communication language; **Epic** uses a different one. [[raw/Alignment_outstanding_subjects_2025-02-19.md]]
- Design intent (Juan/Val): the third COM port is **always the printer**, defaulted so operators don't reassign SAS ports by mistake (SAS 1 must stay primary per Georgia rules; SAS 2 can be reassigned only on a COM 1 malfunction). Pavel to remove/relabel the printer connection in the SAS-port selection UI so the wrong port can't be picked. [[raw/Alignment_outstanding_subjects_2025-02-19.md]]

## Bally iview reporting mismatch (Peach1234)

- On the last test, **Irina** reviewed the **Bally iview** logs and found JVL is correctly matching and sending the right data to Bally, **but Bally's final report is not correct** — JVL doesn't understand how iview produces that report. Val doesn't fully trust the Bally reports (on the Georgia machine he can't select "secure enhance"/"system"). Path forward: build the software, get it to the **Eclipse** lab, and test with the system; Val to call Eclipse. [[raw/Alignment_outstanding_subjects_2025-02-19.md]]

## Peach1234 / DLKG shared build

- Peach1234 and DLKG will use the **same LGS version** to simplify submission to Eclipse, and the same **Progressive Controller** setup approach as the Florida "JPRE" market (an initial-setup screen picks the controller on the network; an **embedded progressive controller** works for a single machine, since Georgia locations usually have one machine). [[raw/Alignment_outstanding_subjects_2025-02-19.md]]
- **Felipe** has the current **Diamondlink** software that supports the printer; the printer work is urgent for him. See [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe Miranda]]. [[raw/Alignment_outstanding_subjects_2025-02-19.md]]

## Release cadence — staged "Peach" packages (Mar 7)

- Georgia will be released in **four staged packages** — **Peach 1, Peach 2, Peach 3, Peach 4** — at roughly **six games at a time**, on ~one-to-two-week intervals, rather than all games at once. Staging is more expensive on **Eclipse** approvals but necessary because [[wiki/people/lena.md|Lena]]'s team is a bottleneck implementing changes across all games. [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]] confirmed the tests passed, so releasing can start. [[raw/game_design_meeting_march_7_2025.md]]
- **Sequencing:** Peach 1 and Peach 2 make up ~**80%** of what's installed in the field; Peach 3 has little install base and Peach 4 isn't installed yet. **Diamond Link** is to be "snuck in" right after Peach 2 (to help sell that machine), then Peach 3 and 4 follow. **Ivan** (release) needs ~**10 days** from starting with Eclipse to issuing a purchase order and submitting the FTP link; Val asked to add a 25% time buffer. [[raw/game_design_meeting_march_7_2025.md]]
- **Pay-table-ID risk:** JVL wanted operators to be able to check the **pay table ID**, but **United** said on a call they **can't** do that — so JVL must take a "leap of faith" that the E9 team's pay table IDs work. Val flagged he dislikes it. [[raw/game_design_meeting_march_7_2025.md]]
- **Old framework for Georgia:** **Jackpot Rush** (and **JPRE**) for Georgia stay on the **old framework** with the **curved-monitor cabinet** targeted for the **summer / gaming show (August)**; only new games go on the new "omni" framework, starting with the Fortune Foo Paws pilot. Keeping new product on the old framework means maintaining Rage Studio and the old stations longer, and the **nudge mechanic** needs updating — parked for a later meeting. [[raw/game_design_meeting_march_7_2025.md]]

## Peach 3/4 "finished but not deployed" (Mar 12)

- On the Mar 12 Gottlieb call, [[wiki/people/michael-gottlieb.md|Michael]] relayed that per Juan and Felipe, only **Peach 1 and Peach 2** are really deployed — **Peach 3 and Peach 4 are finished but not yet in the field** (Jackpot Rush too) — and questioned why Val kept pushing for more product when what's done isn't deployed. [[raw/developing_games_faster_michael_gottlieb_march_12-2025.md]]
- **Val's explanation:** there is **latency between perception and reality** — two large competitors entered the market with worthwhile innovations, which consumed operators' resources (slowing JVL's deployment) and weren't detected immediately; there was also hesitance about the new payment system. He **pressed the brakes at Peach 4** once demand was seen to have dropped, and said in hindsight he would not have pushed Peach 4 — though he'd partly treated it as an "insurance policy" / show-floor bragging rights. Going forward he favours **amortizing the existing library** (e.g. HHR as least-investment/most-revenue, or reviving a 20-year-old "wolves" animal title in class three) over building Peach 5/6. [[raw/developing_games_faster_michael_gottlieb_march_12-2025.md]]

## "Georgia 1" vs "Georgia 2" nudge, and the Nebraska spin-off (Mar 14)

- Val's framing on the Mar 14 Nebraska call: **Georgia 1** is the current **conservative skill nudge**; **Georgia 2** is the **advanced/enhanced skill nudge** the competitors are introducing. The advanced feature is to be deployed onto **Nebraska as it rolls out for Georgia** — acceptance risk being lower than depending on it from launch. [[raw/Application_Nudge_Skill_for_Nebraska_2025-03-14.md]]
- Nebraska reuses the Georgia Peach 1/2/3 software (hands removed, nudge reverted to conservative, other enhancements kept) — the full plan is its own project: [[wiki/projects/nebraska-skill-market.md]] and [[wiki/decisions/2025-03-14-base-nebraska-on-georgia-conservative-nudge.md]]. [[raw/Application_Nudge_Skill_for_Nebraska_2025-03-14.md]]

## New Georgia financial product (Mar 23, from the Doug update)

- Beyond slot machines, JVL developed a **new financial product for Georgia** — **not a slot machine** — built to support **changed Georgia legislation** and **symbiotic** with JVL's slot product. **Mandatory installation is by June 2026.** Val expects purchases to start ~**November 2025** and intensify toward mid-2026. JVL is one of **three players** in that market, with ~**6,000 installations** in play; the machine sells for ~**$12,000**. This is the diversification hedge against the Georgia slot-revenue decline. [[raw/doug_update_jvl-march-23-2025.md]]

[[raw/games_markets-march-24-2025.md]]

## Software-upgrade-kit pricing and financing (Apr 1)

- On an Apr 1 commercial call (Val, [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]], [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan]], [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]], + an unnamed American commercial colleague), JVL decided to **charge for the COAM software-upgrade kits** (the "Peach"/PH1 enhanced software) for the **first time** — retail **~$2,900**, floor **~$2,500**. The kit adds a SAS port, printer integration, all games reworked with the nudge feature, and TITO/legislative compliance, effectively renewing an old cabinet. Full detail (cost basis, competitor comparison, financing strategy, and Val's 2026 kiosk-vacuum market-sizing) on [[wiki/decisions/2025-04-01-price-georgia-coam-upgrade-kits.md]]. [[raw/upgrade_ga-coam_kits-april-1-2025.md]]
- **Financing rule:** kits have a **~zero bill of material**, so JVL finances them generously (12–24 months, zero interest) — but only against **kiosk orders**, and only under a **signed installment contract** (repossession/service-suspension rights); third-party financing (to 5 years) goes through **Elevate**. This complements the Mar-23 financial-product note above (same ~6,000-installation, June-2026-deadline picture). [[raw/upgrade_ga-coam_kits-april-1-2025.md]]

## SAS events 51/52 compliance scare (Apr 14)

On the Apr 14 call ([[wiki/meetings/2025-04-14-sas-51-52-georgia-coam-interpretation.md]]; Val, [[wiki/people/ivan.md|Ivan]], [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina]] translated by [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]], + the American Georgia commercial colleague), Eclipse's **Steve** and GLC's **Chris** flagged that JVL doesn't support **SAS event 51 (hand-pay) and 52 (hand-pay reset)** — mandatory in the SAS 1.8 spec (the transcript says both "from 2003" and "from 2023"; the older date is the spec vintage). [[raw/sas_51_52_requirement_interpretation-april-14-2025.md]]

- **Why it "broke":** Georgia COAM has **no jackpot/hand-pay in practice** — no slot attendant exists to clear a lock, and winnings pay out by **"hands"** (1 hand = 5 spins = 5 credits = **$5**; you can only cash out amount × hands, the rest stays on the machine). Eclipse's Steve used to test 51/52 by setting a **cash-out limit** (e.g. $20), playing to exceed it (5 hands = $25 > $20) so a hand-pay window popped, then turning the key. After JVL **added TITO + AFT support**, that limit can still be set but "will not produce 51 and 52 events over the SAS." Irina: JVL always submitted these hand-pay conditions as **"not applicable"** for Georgia; the concrete bug is that the machine **issues 51 but never sends 52, and keeps re-sending 51**. [[raw/sas_51_52_requirement_interpretation-april-14-2025.md]]
- **The fear:** JVL may have been technically **out of compliance since 2003** even though Eclipse approved and GLC accepted every prior Peach submission — "a delicate conversation." Plan: talk to **Steve first**, then GLC. [[raw/sas_51_52_requirement_interpretation-april-14-2025.md]]
- **Val's reinterpretation (the fix):** jackpot, hand-pay and cash-out are **parallel, not sequential, conditions** — "a hand pay is a jackpot that cannot be paid automatically." His "cup of coffee with a donut" illustration: an operator sets a jackpot/lockup limit (e.g. $1,000) purely so a **buzzer fires (51)** and someone turns the key **(52)** — nothing else changes. So JVL should **allow setting a lockup limit and auto-clear it in the lab** to emit both 51 and 52 and pass certification, even though no operator uses it in the field (no real jackpot exists federally). The team agreed this is a **re-interpretation** ("we misunderstood the requirement," which applies to **all markets**, not just Georgia) and that supporting the jackpot limit is effectively a **new requirement** to write for the COAM product. Class-3 difference recorded: Class 3 has a **$1,200 IRS lockup limit** and does emit 51/52, but on key-turn the jackpot is paid by the house/system; in Georgia the money must return to the **credit meter** for the hands calculation. [[raw/sas_51_52_requirement_interpretation-april-14-2025.md]]
- **FlexPay large-win handling (noted in passing):** if a win exceeds the **$1,000 gift-card max**, funds move to the temporary wallet → FlexPay and card issuance produces **two or more cards** (one $1,000, one for the remainder) — assuming enough hands to cash out. [[raw/sas_51_52_requirement_interpretation-april-14-2025.md]]

## SAS 51/52 — Eclipse Peach-One resolution (Apr 24)

A follow-up session ([[wiki/meetings/2025-04-24-ga-coam-sas-51-52-eclipse-resolution.md]]; Val, [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]], [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina]]) after **Eclipse flagged JVL's "Peach One" submission** — Eclipse tester **Steven** said he can no longer test the hand-pay condition since JVL added **TITO AFT**. Resolution: [[raw/ga-coam_error_51_52-apr-24-2025.md]]

- **Revert to supplying Eclipse two RIS files** — one with **no cash-out limit** and one with a **$20 limit** — so the lab can reproduce the hand-pay/lockup, rather than making the cash-out-limit field user-editable in the field (Val: "the locations never go in and mess with these settings," and making it broadly editable "could be additional issues"). Either approach was deemed acceptable, but double-RIS is less work and urgent. [[raw/ga-coam_error_51_52-apr-24-2025.md]]
- A **hand-pay software fix** (properly emit **51** on lockup and **52** on key-turn/clear when the cash-out limit is set) is "ready right now," internally tested; the rest of the Peach-2 fixes are also ready. Plan: pass Eclipse with **Peach 1** first, then submit the next. Val to get [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]] to retest the AFT transfer with **United** (a ~10-minute test) if available. GLC "clearly said we do have to support these two events regarding a hand pay" — a mandated requirement. [[raw/ga-coam_error_51_52-apr-24-2025.md]]

## BDC financing intro for the financial product (May 20 2025)

On the [[wiki/meetings/2025-05-20-bdc-flexport-ga-coam-financing.md|May-20 BDC call]] Val began arranging capital to finance the Georgia COAM financial-product rollout. He wants **[[wiki/organizations/bdc.md|BDC]]** to finance **JVL itself** (growth capital) so JVL can extend purchase terms to Georgia's many small operators — a different model than the [[wiki/organizations/elevate.md|Elevate]]/[[wiki/organizations/edc.md|EDC]] chain, which approves each operator (impractical across the disaggregated Master License Holders in the ~half-year window). [[raw/bdc_intro_to_flex-port_ga-coam-may-20-2025.md]]

- **Market/financing economics (Val's sizing):** ~**6,000 Georgia locations** × ~**$15,000 equipment/location** = ~**$90M** total market; a **legislative mandate takes effect June 2026** requiring each location to convert fiat→digital onto a prepaid MasterCard, processed by the GLC-sanctioned monopoly **[[wiki/organizations/incom.md|Incom]]** ("Income," a ~$6B US company). JVL currently holds ~**15–20%** of the Georgia skill-game market. Capital ask: ~**$2,000/location** → ~**$12M** for 100% or ~**$6M** at a realistic ~50% share, to generate ~**$45M** revenue over ~3 years (on top of JVL's current ~$8M existing-equipment revenue). Beta testing starts **June 2025**; JVL wants funds by **Jan 1 2026**. [[raw/bdc_intro_to_flex-port_ga-coam-may-20-2025.md]]
- **What JVL learned about BDC:** no revolvers/lines of credit — only **growth capital** (flexible repayment, interest-only, balloon-on-milestones); larger checks (>$1M) come from BDC's **Growth and Transition Capital** team. Full detail on [[wiki/organizations/bdc.md]]; contact [[wiki/people/baba-fakohora.md|Baba Fakohora]]. (This complements the Mar-23 and Apr-1 financial-product notes above — same ~6,000-location, June-2026-deadline picture.) [[raw/bdc_intro_to_flex-port_ga-coam-may-20-2025.md]]

## Class-3-vs-COAM meter interpretation — Seminole-lab retest (May 21 2025)

A certification-testing call ([[wiki/meetings/2025-05-21-ga-coam-class3-meters-seminole-retest.md]]; Val, [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan Martinez]], [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]], the QA engineer "Vlad", [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]]) that extended the SAS 51/52 thread into a broader worry: JVL may be reading Georgia's metering rules **wrong at the root**. (The transcript renders "meter" as **"mitra/MITRA"**, "Georgia COAM" as **"GCOM"/"Georgia COEM"/"Georgia QAM"**, and "Seminole" as **"seminal"** — corrected here, flagged.) The trigger was an **Eclipse** query on the **Total Jackpot Credits** meter, which JVL's Georgia product returns as **0**. [[raw/sas_intricacies_ga-coam-may-21-2025.md]]

- **The root-cause reframing (Val).** Georgia COAM "**was born from Class 3 SAS protocol**" — a "sort of gymnastics to fit Class 3 standard into Georgia COEM" done at the *legislative* level. JVL's mistake was reading the Georgia COAM regulation directly "without any regard to Class 3," when it should interpret **Class-3 first**, then how Georgia COAM fits into it. Correct meters must still **increment** as in Class-3; the hand-pay ("hands") restriction is "just a nuance," not a reason a meter reads 0. [[raw/sas_intricacies_ga-coam-may-21-2025.md]]
- **Jackpot / key-to-credit definitions.** A jackpot is "**any value that is greater than the jackpot limit that we have enabled on the machine**" — with no limit set there is no jackpot (so the meter never fires). Example: limit **$900**, a **$901** win → the machine **locks up** and is cleared by an attendant's **jackpot reset key**. Normally the money then **disappears from the machine** (handled by the system, not the credit meter); the SAS feature **"key-to-credit"** instead moves it to the **credit meter** — a feature JVL supports "**only for C3 class machines. For example, MegaHeat, FireEyes**," **not** for COAM products. Georgia has no jackpots defined today, which is why the Total Jackpot Credits meter gives 0. Adding a jackpot-credit limit "the same as on other products" is doable but "a big alteration," because Georgia's **hands** restriction complicates paying a progressive win. [[raw/sas_intricacies_ga-coam-may-21-2025.md]]
- **Irina's "two steps back."** [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]] raised — and Val had privately shared — the deeper doubt that even JVL's **Class-3 meters may be interpreted incorrectly**; the group agreed to first **redefine/verify the Class-3 meters against the protocol**, then derive the Georgia COAM behaviour. Hand-pay and K2/credit meters were clarified as **different meters** (a hand-pay meter is not the K2/credit-limit meter). [[raw/sas_intricacies_ga-coam-may-21-2025.md]]
- **The undertaking — parallel meter retest.** [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan]] (named "the best expert on Earth" for Class-3 meter interpretation) to **retest meter behaviour on a Class-3 "Mega Hit One" T27/D27 machine at the Seminole ("seminal") lab**, using the **GLI-approved MH1 Class-3 software** JVL had installed there, while **Vlad runs the same software in parallel at JVL** — same version both sides — documenting all jackpot/credit-transfer metering scenarios. [[raw/sas_intricacies_ga-coam-may-21-2025.md]]
- **Lab-subordination / compliance stance.** Val stressed JVL is "**subservient to a laboratory**": if the lab rules JVL wrong, "they can come back, check the previous approvals and revoke them. This will automatically freeze all our games. Instantaneous." He judged the earlier Eclipse pass as luck ("we have good relations, plus this Pavel from Eclipse is not very deep"). Current posture: "**Right now we are compliant. We are behaving as though we're compliant. We are doing our own internal investigations just in case.**" [[raw/sas_intricacies_ga-coam-may-21-2025.md]]
- **Scheduling.** Next call set for **Wed May 28 at 7am** to hear Vlad's findings (Vlad is unavailable from ~May 29 for about a week); an alternative Mon **June 2** was floated, subject to Seminole-lab availability. [[raw/sas_intricacies_ga-coam-may-21-2025.md]]

## Jackpot processing — Class-3 "constitution" vs Georgia-QAM "legislation" (May 28 2025)

The May-28 reconvene ([[wiki/meetings/2025-05-28-jp-jackpot-class3-georgia-coam.md]]; Val, [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan Martinez]], the SAS jackpot/meter expert **[[wiki/people/vladislav-gurin.md|Vladislav Gurin]]** — "Vlad", [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]], and [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]] (owner-confirmed 2026-07-18: meter/QA interpretation → Shishko)) walked jackpot/meter behaviour and produced Val's architectural ruling. The decision-grade content is on [[wiki/decisions/2025-05-28-class3-meters-constitution-georgia-parallel-logic.md]]. [[raw/jp_treatment-may-28-2025.md]]

- **The mechanics clarified.** No jackpot limit → all wins post to **total coin-out**; a jackpot limit (typical US **$1,200**, the IRS value) makes wins ≥ the limit a **hand-pay**. A separate **key-to-credit limit** must be **less than** the jackpot limit and needs a value; a win above the jackpot limit goes **entirely to hand-pay** regardless of key-to-credit. Whether JVL's Seminole test posted to the correct meters is still to be confirmed against the iView/Bally logs (JVL doesn't write meters to its trace logs — noted as a new requirement). [[raw/jp_treatment-may-28-2025.md]]
- **The ruling.** JVL "made Georgia QAM fit" by altering Class-3 meter behaviour — wrong, and a latent compliance risk (if an operator lawfully set a jackpot limit, JVL "would immediately fall out of compliance"). Fix: implement **Class-3 meters/logic purely (the "constitution")**, then a **separate parallel logic** for Georgia-QAM **hands validation of jackpot payouts** (a jackpot evaluates the hand count → partial payout of the qualified amount, remainder stays in hand-pay), never touching the Class-3 meters (the meter set "has been working for decades… like rewriting a fundamental constitution"). **SAS 6.03** (~10 changes) is **not** being adopted for meters. Juan to draft requirements; Irina/Pavel to think it over; reconvene ~10 days. [[raw/jp_treatment-may-28-2025.md]]
- **Georgia has no jackpots today** (amusement, not casino; per Felipe the IRS $1,200 limit doesn't apply), and the jackpot limit doesn't work on the GQL/Class-II products ("it's not C3 yet" — no hand logic). A small jackpot limit could be shipped via RIS for **Eclipse lab demonstration only**, production always no-limit. The Eclipse tester **Steve** is named here in full as **Steve Castro** (the concern: the next tester after him may re-raise the jackpot-limit question). [[raw/jp_treatment-may-28-2025.md]]

## Jackpot / key-to-meter scope gaps found — the reconvene (June 10 2025)

The ~13-days-later reconvene of the May-28 jackpot call ([[wiki/meetings/2025-06-10-jackpot-meter-sas-scope-gaps.md]]; Val, [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan Martinez]], the SAS/meter expert **[[wiki/people/vladislav-gurin.md|Vlad]]**, [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]] (owner-confirmed 2026-07-18: meter/QA → Shishko), [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel/Pasha]], and an implementer "Zhenya"). Trigger: during JVL's **PH1 submission**, **Eclipse** noted that JVL's **jackpot limit** — unlike other machines — **can't be changed for lab testing** (JVL confirmed the jackpot limit "does not function the way it functions in Class 3" for Georgia COAM software). The call drilled the Class-3 **key-to-meter / jackpot-limit** behaviour and uncovered concrete scope gaps. [[raw/meter_sas_function-june-10-2025.md]]

- **The educational baseline (Juan, 30 yrs gaming ops).** A **jackpot limit** (typical US **$1,200**, an **IRS/federal tax mandate** on wins ≥$1,200) triggers a **hand-pay lockup**. Three ways it resolves: (1) **standard hand-pay** — attendant turns the reset key, the value is wiped from the machine and paid by the casino system (no voucher in system-validation; player waits); (2) **allow-hand-pay-receipt** — the key prints a jackpot ticket and instantly returns the machine to play (Seminole practice); (3) **key-to-credit / key-to-meter** — the win is transferred to the **credit meter** (via a physical key turn, or, on advanced systems like **Bally SDS**, a player self-transfer on the tracking display). In **Georgia it can only be key-to-meter** (money must return to the credit meter for the hands calculation). [[raw/meter_sas_function-june-10-2025.md]]
- **Scope gap #1 — key-to-meter can't be set higher than the jackpot limit.** Juan established that **key-to-credit is a local casino operations choice (risk/AML), separate from the federal tax jackpot limit**, and can be set **higher** than the jackpot limit (Hard Rock uses ~$1,200 main-floor + $10,000–$50,000 high-limit). JVL had **implemented the key-to-meter limit as forced-lower-than the jackpot limit** — Irina defended it as correct, Val/Juan ruled it a **scope gap**: the two must be **independent values**. [[raw/meter_sas_function-june-10-2025.md]]
- **Scope gap #2 — no partial transfer against a key-to-credit limit.** The key-to-credit limit is a **threshold** (born from AML; kiosk single-transactions capped ~$3,000): a win above it should transfer **only up to the limit** to the credit meter, leaving the remainder as hand-pay. JVL doesn't do this **partial transfer** today. [[raw/meter_sas_function-june-10-2025.md]]
- **Scope gap #3 — no lab test for the mechanical key-to-meter case, and a possible double-count.** JVL has **no test case** where a win above the jackpot limit is sent to the credit meter by a mechanical key turn, and the team is unsure whether that path **double-posts** (jackpot meter + hand-pay meter + credit meter) — which would make machines look like they **overpaid / are losing**. Because **Seminole machines have used key-to-credit for ~2 years**, this "could be affecting our numbers." [[raw/meter_sas_function-june-10-2025.md]]
- **Testing-rig limitation (Vlad).** JVL can only exercise this through a **~20-year-old IGT "SAS Tool" emulator** (a SAS *tool*, not a full SAS *host*), with truncated functionality and no player-tracking screen — so many host-side flows can't be reproduced. Val's counter: the mechanical key-to-meter path is a **mechanical process needing no SAS command**, so JVL should be able to bench-test it directly. [[raw/meter_sas_function-june-10-2025.md]]
- **Action.** Team to write up all the options, build a **flow diagram**, verify the behaviour and implement (Zhenya to implement the independent/higher key-to-meter + partial transfer). **Class 3 must be made correct first** (Georgia layered on per the [[wiki/decisions/2025-05-28-class3-meters-constitution-georgia-parallel-logic.md|constitution/legislation ruling]]). Next meeting **Friday 10:30**. [[raw/meter_sas_function-june-10-2025.md]]

## Jackpot / key-to-meter flowchart review (June 12 2025)

The next-day continuation of the June-10 scope-gap call ([[wiki/meetings/2025-06-12-jackpot-meter-flowchart-review.md]]; Val, [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan]], SAS/meter expert [[wiki/people/vladislav-gurin.md|Vlad]], [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]] (owner-confirmed 2026-07-18: meter/QA → Shishko)). Vlad walked the **future-state flowchart** of Class-3 jackpot / key-to-meter behaviour that he built as the June-10 undertaking. (Nebraska was asked to delay their call so this could run on.) [[raw/jp_meters-june-12-2025.md]]

- **The variants walked.** (1) No key-to-meter limit, jackpot limit $1,200 → any win above the limit starts the **jackpot hand-paid dialogue**; turn the key, all money goes to hand-pay. (2) key-to-meter limit **equal to** the jackpot limit — "how it works today," but Juan called it **unrealistic** (an operator would never set key-to-credit exactly equal to the jackpot limit). (3) jackpot limit $1,200, **key-to-meter $25,000**: a $5,000 win shows the **same existing jackpot/hand-pay dialogue** ("congratulations… please call attendant" — Val/Juan ruled **do not build a new dialogue**, reuse the one players/staff already know), then on the audit-key turn the $5,000 transfers to the **credit meter / balance** ("key-to-meter" / "win to balance"); there is **no individual meter** tracking it. (4) hard case — a **$30,000** win above both limits: **$25,000 (the key-to-meter limit) transfers to the credit meter** on the first key turn, the remaining **$5,000 stays hand-pay** on a second key turn; the **dialogue shows twice** (both need casino intervention). [[raw/jp_meters-june-12-2025.md]]
- **Ticket vs lockup / tax.** Under **secure-enhanced validation** the second key turn **prints a jackpot ticket** and releases the machine; under **system-validation only** there is no ticket, so the machine stays **locked until the attendant returns with cash**. Tax is on the **original $30,000** (the $1,200 is exempt), so **$28,800 taxable at 25%** (28% "foreign tax" for non-US citizens). [[raw/jp_meters-june-12-2025.md]]
- **The win-calculation double-posting worry (Juan).** In the "win to balance" flow the transaction posts to **both the coin-out meter and jackpot credits**, so Juan wants the **net-win formula** confirmed (coin-in − coin-out − jackpots = net win) to be sure JVL isn't "double dipping." The intended handling: the $25,000 posts to **both coin-in and coin-out (zeroing out)** because it's addressed via the jackpot meter, while the full **$30,000** posts to the **jackpot meter**. Because there has been "**only one jackpot in the last 18 months**," any error is invisible to operators — but JVL may have **reported a machine as losing more than it did**. Juan to send a sheet illustrating the formula. [[raw/jp_meters-june-12-2025.md]]
- **Total-jackpot-credit meter gap.** Today the **Total Jackpot Credits** meter reflects only the **attendant-paid** portion (what's requested over SAS), not the **key-to-meter balance** amounts — so a $30,000 win shows less than $30,000. Val wants the total-jackpot meter to reflect the **full** jackpot value. [[raw/jp_meters-june-12-2025.md]]
- **The blocking undertaking — a block diagram of every meter.** The team stalled ("we're all staring at each other… a vacuum") over which meter increments in the key-to-meter case — there is no agreed **credit-in meter** name. Val made it an undertaking: **Vlad to produce a block diagram of every single meter JVL has (maximum, proper names, whether used or not)** — "we cannot move forward without it" — for a **Tuesday 11 am** meeting (~June 17; Vlad to send invites). [[raw/jp_meters-june-12-2025.md]]

## Georgia content deprioritized at the June-12 games review

On the [[wiki/meetings/2025-06-12-games-design-priorities-review.md|June-12 games-design priorities review]] (Val, [[wiki/people/michael-gottlieb.md|Michael]], [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan]], [[wiki/people/lena.md|Lena]], [[wiki/people/efim-podvoiski.md|Efim]]) the group concluded Georgia is well-stocked on content and pulled back new Georgia game work: [[raw/games_design-june-12-2025.md]]

- **Jackpot Rush for Georgia deprioritized.** It had lived in the system for months because it was to be featured on the **Curve Cabinet** at the **August Georgia show** — but the Curve Cabinet is **not available**, and Michael judged **[[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]] needs no games this summer** (the market is consumed complying with the new legislation). So Jackpot Rush for Georgia is shifted back. (This continues the [[#curved-monitor-delay--release-status-may-1-2025|May-1]] curved-cabinet delay — the August show now won't feature it.) Its **award structure differs from the Class-3 version** (in line with other Georgia products, not a copy). [[raw/games_design-june-12-2025.md]]
- **Diamond Link (DLK) Georgia is the premier August item instead.** DLK is **already installed in the field**; the show build only changes **nudges and 2v2** and "doesn't have to be 100%… just show software," so it can be ready by August (QA can test the changes fast). Val: "I don't believe that Georgia needs any content" — **Peach 1/2/3** are ready (new Peach 1 with the new nudge in-field "next week"; Peach 2 approved; Peach 3 submitted to the regulator — "Eclipse is moving very fast"). Michael wants to **track progressive payouts (even sub-IRS amounts)** with Pavel to be sure games aren't over-paying. [[raw/games_design-june-12-2025.md]]

## Class-3 meter test review + Georgia-QAM flow deferred (June 17 2025)

The Tuesday-11am follow-up to the [[#jackpot--key-to-meter-flowchart-review-june-12-2025|June-12 flowchart review]] ([[wiki/meetings/2025-06-17-class3-meters-georgia-qam-flow.md]]; [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], the SAS/meter expert [[wiki/people/vladislav-gurin.md|Vlad]], [[wiki/people/ivan.md|Ivan]], [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]], and [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]] translating (owner-confirmed 2026-07-18: meter/QA → Shishko)). [[raw/Metering_class_3-june-17-2025.md]]

- **Class-3 meter tests 1 and 2 passed.** Vlad presented his test results — an Excel sheet plus screenshots of the meters. **Test 3 could not be run:** per Irina's instruction the casino cannot initiate it, because the casino has **disabled the ability to send the SAS command that clears a hand-pay remotely** (a security measure). On tests 1 and 2 the group agreed the meters "look right." [[raw/Metering_class_3-june-17-2025.md]]
- **Vlad's ~300-meter table (the June-12 undertaking) is done.** Vlad "created a big table with all meters… about 300 meters"; the team agreed not to review all of them on the call. This discharges the June-12 block-diagram commitment. [[raw/Metering_class_3-june-17-2025.md]]
- **Key-to-meter behaviour still to be fixed (reaffirms the June-10 scope gaps).** The remaining problem: the **key-to-meter should be able to be set higher than the jackpot limit**, and when the jackpot exceeds the credit meter it must be treated as a **partial transaction** — so the logic must be changed to support a partial transfer. [[raw/Metering_class_3-june-17-2025.md]]
- **Georgia-QAM flow diagram deferred to a dedicated meeting.** With Class-3 metering broadly confirmed, Val's open concern is how the (revised) key-to-meter behaviour transplants onto **Georgia-QAM** logic (the hand-paid-jackpot → credit-meter transfer, and what can/can't be transferred out) — "this is where a flow diagram is needed." Since it can't be done on the fly, a **separate Georgia-QAM flow meeting** was set for **the following Monday** (after 10am, same slot as the call). For Georgia the partial key-to-credit case will be rare — the plan is to keep the key-to-credit limit very high ($20k–$50k) so the full amount transfers via the hands process. [[raw/Metering_class_3-june-17-2025.md]]

## Phased Tito/FlexPOS beta rollout schedule (June 18 2025)

On the [[wiki/meetings/2025-06-18-gcoam-ticket-redemption-workflow.md|June-18 ticket-redemption session]] ([[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/nathan-carver.md|Nathan Carver]], [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]], [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi Peari]]) Val laid out a phased beta plan at JVL's own registered Georgia warehouse/showroom location (3 machines — **1 JVL + 2 competitor**, all with Muda-Goose boards + a printer + a POS). The device/architecture detail is on [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]]. (§12: transcript internally duplicates — facts once.) [[raw/about_tickets_redemption-gcoam-june-18–2025.md]]

- **The compliance clock (restated).** Georgia prohibits gambling (legalized via the "skill" reel-realignment fiction ~20 years ago) and cash payouts; the new law converts wins to a **prepaid MasterCard, mandatory June 1 2026** (per this June-18 source; **later Aug-14 sources state July 1 2026** — the effective date is evolving across sources, latest-source-wins, not a contradiction; owner ruling 2026-07-20: exact date not critical yet, will stabilize). **3–5 players** are gearing up redemption schemes. A master license holder (multiple machines, multiple locations; Georgia bans vertical integration between master/manufacturer/location) with ~1,000 machines faces a "crazy investment" — JVL's play is a **"Lego kit"** letting an operator do the cheapest Mutagoose-POS compliance or grow into a full Tito infrastructure, "to practically monopolize the market." [[raw/about_tickets_redemption-gcoam-june-18–2025.md]]
- **The phased beta (Val's schedule).** **Sept 1** — beta 1: the most primitive POS (communicates only to Incom), manual ticket-value entry, validate card, 3 machines. **Sept 15** — beta 2: wedge the POS between Muda-Goose and the printer, capture values, cashier selects. **Sept 30** — step 3: connect the **Flex Tower** to the POS, card dispensed through the tower. **Oct 15** — engage the **Tito** concept: machines retrofitted with printers, Tito server in the location, Tower + POS on **WSS protocol**, JVL SMIB on the second SAS port. **Dec 15** — present the full "financial solution infrastructure" (the Lego-kit catalog). [[raw/about_tickets_redemption-gcoam-june-18–2025.md]]
- **FlexPOS is now priority #1, FlexTower #2.** Val redirected Nathan to POS; a "beta release" = a live in-house test with Incom validating a Move-the-Goose ticket through a JVL FlexPOS. [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan Martinez]] booked a Friday **FlexTower/POS mock-up-screens** meeting. [[raw/about_tickets_redemption-gcoam-june-18–2025.md]]
- **June 19 — Sept-1 beta scope confirmed.** The [[wiki/meetings/2025-06-19-flex-pos-use-case-design.md|next-morning Flex POS session]] wrote the "Manual Ticket Redemption for FlexPOS" use case for the Sept-1 beta (the handshake between FlexPOS and Incom + the $2-fee deduction, at the registered warehouse location); Tito/barcode validation and OCR are out of MVP. [[raw/flex_POS-use_case_architecture_requirements-june-19-2025.md]]

## Manufacturing/assembly relocates to Georgia (June 24–26 2025)

Beyond the software/COAM product, Georgia becomes JVL's **manufacturing base**: JVL decided to move machine assembly out of Canada to a new **10,500 sq ft freestanding warehouse in Louisville** (Atlanta area; up from the ~3,000 sq ft Norcross unit), with [[wiki/people/elizabeth.md|Elizabeth]] heading manufacturing and Echo + Flex production shipping there over ~30 days. Full decision + rationale (tariff risk; DDP quotes; no Business Central manufacturing module) on [[wiki/decisions/2025-06-24-move-manufacturing-from-canada-to-georgia.md]]. [[raw/Alignment_procurement_manufacturing_transfer-june-26-2025.md]]

## GACOAM trade-show recap (Aug 22 2025)

On the [[wiki/meetings/2025-08-22-gacoam-trade-show-recap.md|Aug-22 7:09 PM recap]] ([[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan Martinez]], [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe Miranda Giorgi]]) Val had Juan and Felipe walk their notes from the **Georgia COAM (GACOAM) gaming show** while he listened. [[raw/GACOAM_trade_show_recap.md]]

- **Show + JVL booth.** ~**500 attendees** (vendors + masters). JVL was directly across from **Aristocrat**, drew comparable traffic, and displayed more machines. Juan estimated **6–7 machine orders** closed and **at least 3–4 operators** signing up for the year-end **FlexPay trial**. Exhibitors named: JVL, Vanilla, Aristocrat, Light & Wonder, Novomatic, Konami, and an unusually large **C3 Gaming** booth (a vendor whose product has historically failed on-site). Customers praised JVL's "classic casino look" and that JVL showed **both TITO and e-wallet**. [[raw/GACOAM_trade_show_recap.md]]
- **Competitor intel.** **Light & Wonder** offered a **"2-for-1"** — a free second software set (Ultimate Fire Link, Lion Link, Huff & Puff) that existing cabinet owners self-install from USBs; the sales pitch is "here are brand-new games free," and Juan/Felipe judged only ~5% of operators would actually take it. **Aristocrat** introduced a **vertical cabinet** (small, cost-reduced monitor) and showed a **wheel-bonus + three-pot persistence** game — casino bonusing coming to the market; masters called going with Aristocrat "a no-brainer." **IGT** showed an oversized ~**55-inch curved-monitor** cabinet with a topper — too big for a convenience store. General feedback: machines are "getting taller every year." [[raw/GACOAM_trade_show_recap.md]]
- **Next-year booth ideas (Val).** Regretted not showing **ECHO** machines; wants a **bar presentation** (physical bar, bartender, ~3 machines with bar paraphernalia) to open the **bar-environment** expansion — but built around the **Flex countertop** (single screen + feedback, larger format) with a complementary **Flex podium**, a **Class-B machine priced ~$6,500** (range $6,500–$8,000). Reconsider showing **toppers** next year given the IGT lesson. Prepare **full-page ads** and sign-up forms / DocuSign contracts (lawyer-vetted) for a full sales push in **January** (not November — JVL "will not be ready for November"; Jan–Jul 2026 is the "frantic time"). [[raw/GACOAM_trade_show_recap.md]]
- **Upgrade-kit pricing stance (reaffirms [[wiki/decisions/2025-04-01-price-georgia-coam-upgrade-kits.md|the Apr-1 pricing decision]]).** Nothing free now that JVL is established — **keep the ~$2,900 retail** as a **bargaining sheet**, and **pair conversions with machine/POS sales as "marketing dollars"** (e.g. buy a new machine → get 1–2 free upgrades, a perceived ~$3,000 discount). The redesigned games' math is **unchanged / true to the originals**; customers recognize them as Peach 1/2/3, a **Peach 4** is pending, and three-reel low-line games remain important. [[raw/GACOAM_trade_show_recap.md]]
- **The Eilers floor-performance reporting play (Val's strategy).** JVL should **report performance selectively** — the new software is rolling out on ~50–100 of ~2,000 machines, so JVL submits numbers from only the **~5 best machines** running it, in the **best locations**, to earn a top ranking. Tactics: place JVL machines in prime spots next to a bar (not a toilet), keep them **spotless with a chair**, and **incentivize cooperating operators** with free service / upgrades / parts / even a free machine to report extraordinary numbers. Rationale Val gave: **Eilers has no direct Intralot access** — manufacturers submit their own data — and Eilers favors manufacturers because manufacturer advertising, not fairness, funds it. Tagline Val wants JVL to own: **"JVL is a no-brainer."** (Recorded as Val's stated commercial strategy.) [[raw/GACOAM_trade_show_recap.md]]
- **Distributor / "tech-ambassador" commission model (proposed).** Turn the master operators' **service technicians / "ambassadors"** into JVL's official **distributors**: when one signs up a location, a **commission (~20% of the equipment-cost fee, e.g. of a ~$2/day charge)** goes to the signer for the contract's duration — the **transactional fees stay a company secret**. Landscape: ~**6,500 Georgia locations**, ~50% serviced by a handful of large companies (United, Lucky Bucks, Arc, Bulldog, Inamax); ~2–3 of them are signable as distributors (some already have their own solution), the rest via ~5 ambassadors — no exclusivity, mirroring InComm's own posture. Channel detail on [[wiki/topics/flexpay-mobile-app-and-service-advocates.md]]. [[raw/GACOAM_trade_show_recap.md]]
- **Flex-POS "Trojan-horse" pilot.** 3–4 customers showed interest in a Flex pilot; Val's plan is to tell them they're testing the **kiosk** but **substitute and lead them toward testing the Flex-POS wedging into the Mother Goose** infrastructure (easier reconciliation). JVL will tell operators how many locations to pilot, not the reverse. [[raw/GACOAM_trade_show_recap.md]]
- **Sales forecast (Felipe).** Working ~**120 games**; ~**15–20 masters/operators**; **106–140 cabinets** expected before year-end (Epic Amusement may reorder 30–40 more). Val holds a **buffer stock in Toronto**, unshipped pending calmer tariff/customs processing. Val asked Felipe to load all deals into the sales funnel. [[raw/GACOAM_trade_show_recap.md]]

(Device-preference signals from the same recap — e-wallet preferred over TITO, printer-retrofit non-starter, RIS limits until the 2026 framework, the redemption-center third stage — are on [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]].)

On the [[wiki/meetings/2025-09-18-games-concepts-markets-review.md|Sept-18 games/markets review]] ([[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]] + [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan]]): **MG Oil agreed higher bets** — min 40 credits on three games, 50 on the other three — deploying Monday via [[wiki/people/denis-desmarais.md|Dennis]] (new install file already sent).
