Val is explicitly unsure which product goes first on NOVA; see [[wiki/topics/nova-platform-architecture.md]] (Aug-26). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Alignment_Planning_NOVA_and_Sept_Releases-August-26-2026.md]]

## Aug 26 2026 — the ATM ambition meets InComm's red line, and the licensing precondition

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-26-incomm-skill-pay-atm-guidance.md|Aug-26 3:00 PM ET InComm follow-up]] (English; Val, [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]], [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan Martinez]] / InComm's Jason Hall, Allen Lee, Adam Harlow) set the boundary for what JVL can put into Georgia locations. Card-product and posture detail on [[wiki/organizations/incom.md]] and [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-26-no-cash-from-coam-redemption-device-for-skill-pay.md]]; the two-product commitment is [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-26-certify-only-flex-iq-and-flex-port-with-incomm.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_InComm_Skill_Pay_ATM_Guidance-August-26-2026.md]]

- **Val confirmed the ATM ambition and its precondition in the same breath.** "I'm confirming that we are working on an ATM. Let's call it ATM Redemption Service. This cannot be, I repeat, cannot be implemented. Without us being licensed as an ATM operator. Which requires a bank." On InComm's own suggestion JVL contacted **Harris Bank** — "we're in touch with them. But aside from my initial conversation, nothing happened." Until then, "we are not certified as an ATM operator or ATM provider… And because we're not an ATM provider, we cannot have that solution." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Skill_Pay_ATM_Guidance-August-26-2026.md]]
- **His stated reason is proportionality, not compliance theatre.** "We are not in the business of breaking the law… because it's going to cost us probably our business and 43 year of tedious work. So we're not going to jeopardize it over a potential market in Georgia." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Skill_Pay_ATM_Guidance-August-26-2026.md]]
- **Val's market argument, which InComm acknowledged but did not accept.** "in fact, the ATMs are already in all locations… every single location has an ATM machine," and today's ATM charges make that business "either crippled or expensive." JVL's standalone ATM "resolves the issue of cost, not legitimacy, not legality, because it's already there" — the saving coming from "Removing the aspect of a fill." Jason Hall: "I understand your point. I do. It makes sense. From you know a technology and efficiency and solutions perspective" — but Georgia's gaming regulators have not addressed it, "and I suspect it would be viewed negatively at this point." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Skill_Pay_ATM_Guidance-August-26-2026.md]]
- **The commercial dependency Val named out loud:** "the moment you stop supplying cards to us, we're not going to be in business." Jason's guidance is that InComm "would stop offering our products to someone who who tried in 2026 or 2027 to combine an ATM with a Georgia COAM redemption kiosk." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Skill_Pay_ATM_Guidance-August-26-2026.md]]
- **A structural feature of the Georgia COAM market surfaced.** InComm ships its cards to the **location owner**, not to JVL and not to the master operator — Val: "They don't ship cards to JVL. JVL does not touch the cards. The master operator does not touch the cards. But the location owner… Gets the cards. Dispenses the cards and has an ATM network that they own, that they actually bought." Juan added that the Georgia Lottery "was very anti-regulating kiosks… hence you're dealing with, you know, 20, 30 different products because they decided not to regulate it." Neither point was resolved on the call. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Skill_Pay_ATM_Guidance-August-26-2026.md]]
- **Commission and inducement.** The commission structure carries over unchanged from Prize Pay to Skill Pay while InComm recovers first-year setup costs, and a **product amendment to JVL's reseller agreement** is required before Skill Pay can be activated. Val asked whether JVL could hand 100% of its commission to the location; Jason had no objection provided the activation fee stays static, but Juan flagged a possible **GLC inducement** problem and took the clarification. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Skill_Pay_ATM_Guidance-August-26-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear across both Aug-26 sessions — market, card-programme and device economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Aug 26 2026 — route consolidation: why JVL cannot tell who owns its installed cabinets

A six-minute car briefing before a customer visit ([[wiki/meetings/2026-08-26-vishal-patel-pre-meeting-briefing.md|Aug-26 12:37 PM ET]]; [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]] briefing [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]]) surfaced a structural feature of the Georgia market that runs underneath the whole install base. Account detail on [[wiki/people/vishal-patel.md]].

- **The buy-grow-sell route model.** [[wiki/people/vishal-patel.md|Vishal Patel]] — once a heavy JVL buyer, "ten at a time, twenty at a time" around 2023–24 (Felipe's hedged dating) — runs what Felipe reads as a route-flipping business: "grab locations, make them a bit profitable and sell… get them small, make them grow and then sell them **four times the price**. You know with machines included." Felipe is explicit that he does not fully understand it: "I don't I don't under clearly understand what his business model is." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Vishal_Patel_Pre_Meeting_Briefing-August-26-2026.md]]
- **The consolidators on the buy side are United Gaming and Diamond Amusement.** "a lot of the cabinets that I have sold to him, I have reconfigured or upgraded or changed or fixed **for United Gaming**. United has bought a lot of his routes, uh **Diamond Amusement** and others." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Vishal_Patel_Pre_Meeting_Briefing-August-26-2026.md]]
- **What it costs JVL: install-base blindness and unpaid rework.** Because cabinets move with the locations, JVL cannot tell which machines an original buyer still holds — "It's been very hard for us to know what date still keep. From what they originally bought from us" (garbled sentence; the sense is clear, the wording is not). The visible work is reconfiguration and upgrade of JVL cabinets for whoever bought the route; the latest instance was "ten cabinets for repair. We repaired them in house, got them all ready and then they picked them up. We upgraded all all the software that they bring." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Vishal_Patel_Pre_Meeting_Briefing-August-26-2026.md]]
- **The lapsed-customer pattern.** Vishal "hasn't purchased in a while," still has "some JVLs in the warehouse," has lost his own technician (Padma) to a third-party tech service firm, and is reached through an administrator, **Angie**, who "always calls me when she has a problem." Felipe won the Aug-26 appointment by pitching the **transactional products**, not the games: "The owner of JVL is here… Games, but transactional products." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Vishal_Patel_Pre_Meeting_Briefing-August-26-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — no named-person compensation in this source; route resale multiples are deal economics.

## Aug 26 2026 — a customer visit puts the whole argument in one room: "nobody's really asking for JVL"

The 1:00 PM visit to [[wiki/organizations/narayani-amusements.md|Narayani Amusements]] ([[wiki/meetings/2026-08-26-narayani-amusements-trade-in-negotiation.md|Aug-26 12:53–1:28 PM ET]]; [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/vishal-patel.md|Vishal Patel]] and colleagues, [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]]) is the field version of the argument the Georgia thread has been having internally all month. The deal struck is [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-26-narayani-three-machine-d27-trade-in-pilot.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Narayani_Amusements_Trade_In_Negotiation-August-26-2026.md]]

- **The operator's verdict, unvarnished.** Asked what it would take to buy regularly: "honestly speaking, Right now, every location that we have they ask for aristocrats… **So nobody's really asking for [JVL] right now**." His explanation is payout: Aristocrat and Light & Wonder win locations "by setting a very high payout center… their payouts are like **ninety one ninety two percent**" — which he says he personally dislikes, "But it's the name of the game… the flow is going that way." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Narayani_Amusements_Trade_In_Negotiation-August-26-2026.md]]
- **Val's counter, stated as manufacturer arithmetic, not opinion.** "We actually pay out more. The reason you make less money is because the player likes us more… the player actually benefits more from us. But it's not about the player. It's about how much money you and the location make… So in the new machine, we pulled back the payout, We pulled back the RTP in order to give you more money because the player really does not see the difference." The bet-level half: "Your machine's minimum bet is between forty and fifty cents on one or two games… Our games cost twenty five cents. That is another reason why you make less money." Full argument on [[wiki/topics/slot-game-design-principles.md]] (Aug 27 2026, where he puts numbers on it). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Narayani_Amusements_Trade_In_Negotiation-August-26-2026.md]]
- **The new-games positioning: head-to-head, 40% cheaper.** "**My new game designer comes from Light and Wonder**. So I know that the new games that I'm bringing to the market are very well competitive to Li[ght] and Wonder, and we're priced approximately **40% cheaper**. So your return on investment will be spectacular," and "the new games, we are setting up **exactly like** [Light] and wonders and aristocrat, **same payouts, same minimum bet**… You will end up making much more money because we're forty fifty percent cheaper." Val also priced the competition out loud — "But they're forty percent more expensive. Is it at **eighteen thousand**?" — against his own **$14,500** D43. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Narayani_Amusements_Trade_In_Negotiation-August-26-2026.md]]
- **Competitor financing is the other lever JVL is up against.** Narayani self-finances but takes manufacturer terms on top; the example given was a deal just made with IGT at the show — "a fifteen month deal" — with variants like "ten days of no payments and then nine months of payment" and "Twelve months of payments plus three months of no payments." This is the same gap Felipe reported after GAMOA, where JVL's two-month grace period "was not compelling enough." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Narayani_Amusements_Trade_In_Negotiation-August-26-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Machine_Sales_Strategy_Post_GOAM-August-21-2026.md]]
- **What a six-machine Georgia location looks like from the operator's side:** one Aristocrat, one Light & Wonder, two more (the transcript renders them "IGS", "Photon box", "Prizm Sight"), and the sixth decision now going to a brand rendered "Bad Dog" because "Fusion has been not been that great again." Val did not recognise the latter name. The brand renderings are not resolved — see [[wiki/organizations/narayani-amusements.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Narayani_Amusements_Trade_In_Negotiation-August-26-2026.md]]
- **Trade-in becomes the way back into a lapsed account.** ~25 idle JVL D27s bought at $13,200 each were converted into a live 3-machine pilot, with the full swap held hostage to installing JVL's transactional equipment across the route. The route-consolidation blindness recorded below is the reason JVL did not already know what he held. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Narayani_Amusements_Trade_In_Negotiation-August-26-2026.md]]

**Back-reference (§11), Aug 11 2026.** The operator listed as "**Mariani Amusement**" in the Aug-11 pipeline **notes** (2 trial cabinets, one of only two operators the trial had been "firmly discussed" with) is named "**Narayani**" by Val in the **transcript** of that same call, in the same list positions. Recorded as an **inference**, with "Mariani" kept as an alias on [[wiki/organizations/narayani-amusements.md]]; the Aug-11 entry above is left as written. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GA_COAM_Pipeline_Trial_Forecast-August-11-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_GA_COAM_Pipeline_Trial_Forecast-August-11-2026.md]]

## Aug 27 2026 — the Georgia Lottery moves on gift-card cash-back commissions, and Val's case against the industry reports

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-27-halcyon-amusements-recycler-pitch.md|Aug-27 3:03 PM ET operator visit]] ([[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/yash-patel.md|Yash Patel]] of [[wiki/organizations/halcyon-amusements.md|Halcyon Amusements]], [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]]) the operator opened his phone and showed Val a regulatory letter. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Halcyon_Amusements_Recycler_Pitch_and_Lottery_Notice-August-27-2026.md]]

- **The notice.** "the **Georgia Lottery** sent this notice out **a couple of weeks ago**. And what they're asking the retailers to do with a gift card system is… **end of day report from their register, their bank account statements, and how much commission they're charging** to transact back off of a gift card when they're doing cash back. So the lottery has realized that." Val asked for a copy on the spot and the operator undertook to send it. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Halcyon_Amusements_Recycler_Pitch_and_Lottery_Notice-August-27-2026.md]]
- **What the lottery has realised — the commission stack.** "the lottery first told us. **Chris Plonic** at the lottery told us, **as soon as the retailer issues a gift card, we don't care what happens to it.** But then what they realized is. Now the retailer is issuing a card. [InComm] is taking **two dollars fifty cents** to transact that card. Now the retailer to do cash back is saying, 'Hey, we'll take **five dollars or ten dollars or thirty dollars**.' And at some locations, when they win a big prize, the players come in like, 'I've got a thousand dollars on this card. **Keep fifty dollars. Keep a hundred dollars.** I'll give me cash.' Now the lottery's like, 'Oh man, we're missing out on this money. How do we come back in?' Because **the lottery's not loyal to anybody**." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Halcyon_Amusements_Recycler_Pitch_and_Lottery_Notice-August-27-2026.md]]
- **Neither man saw a defence.** Val: "And It will continue. It will… There is nothing anybody could do. Nothing. Nothing at all. Just put up with the shit." The operator: "It's part of the rodeo. It's their circus, and we're their clowns… every state is the same." This sits directly against the [[wiki/organizations/incom.md|InComm]] card economics and the Aug-26 ATM red line recorded above. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Halcyon_Amusements_Recycler_Pitch_and_Lottery_Notice-August-27-2026.md]]
- **Val's case against the Eilers report, stated to a customer.** Shown a GGR/install-base report at the meeting ("Here's the number one game… **Thirty one hundred units** install base. Total growth that they're doing **seventeen percent**" — figures as the operator read them aloud, source publication garbled in the transcript), Val's answer: "**These reports are incorrect.** I know there is a mathematical, gross mathematical error. I could show you reports where, for a period of time, they claim that **we paid out a million dollars**… The **Eiler** report is a **self-proclaiming** report." And on why placement is not neutral: "they also charge you for advertising. So if I'm a good customer, which IGT Enlightened Wanderers, their prime customer, they will always put them in a **prime spot**." *("Enlightened Wanderers" is transcribed as written; read in context as Light & Wonder — an inference, not a corrected quote.)* This is the same firm the wiki has carried as **Eilers** since 2025 and as "ILERS" in the Aug-21 transcript — the identification is an inference from role and context. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Halcyon_Amusements_Recycler_Pitch_and_Lottery_Notice-August-27-2026.md]]
- **"For this reason alone" — the modem and the data buy-back.** Val ran straight from the report critique into the September-1 LTE modem and the $337/year/machine data repurchase. Program detail on [[wiki/topics/machine-data-repurchase-program.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Halcyon_Amusements_Recycler_Pitch_and_Lottery_Notice-August-27-2026.md]]
- **A small field datum.** A Halcyon location in **Lilburn** already runs **two JVLs**, the second put in by removing a competitor cabinet the operator called "SkyRiser… Banilla." The location's cap is nine machines. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Halcyon_Amusements_Recycler_Pitch_and_Lottery_Notice-August-27-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear across both Aug-26 and Aug-27 customer visits — machine prices, trade-in values, commissions and operator economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Aug 31 2026 — ARC Gaming: the market map from a #2–3 master, and the GLC's data letter

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-31-arc-gaming-transactional-demo-class-a.md|Aug-31 10:04–11:45 AM ET ARC Gaming demo]] ([[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/georgios-zazanis.md|George "Zaza" Zazanis]] of [[wiki/organizations/arc-gaming.md|ARC Gaming]], [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]], [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]]) produced the most candid operator read of the Georgia master landscape in the corpus. ARC's own operation, the kiosk lock-in and the Class A material live on [[wiki/organizations/arc-gaming.md]] and [[wiki/topics/georgia-class-a-machines.md]]; what follows is the market intelligence. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]

- **United Gaming is failing, and the pattern has run before.** "United they go in a very weird situation. **The rumor has it that Bandi, which is the owner, is getting bankrupt, and they're taking the company out of him.**" Val: "That's what I heard too." Zazanis traced the same shape at **Lucky Bucks** first: its previous owner (rendered "Alan Millan") "got a lot of money from the banks, **like 300 million**. The money disappeared magically. The day it disappeared, [Lucky Bucks] went from being the top… and **United started getting all the locations**. After a few years, exactly the same happened. United got a big loan… The money disappeared. United started losing money." The consequence JVL cares about: "I know some of the top locations of United, they're **moving to other masters**… He's moving top tier locations from United to somebody else." Val: "United is going to go down." Zazanis: "It's going… They say that [the owner] might stay on the board. They might put somebody else to manage it." *(The owner's name is rendered "Bandi"/"Bundy" here; this wiki has carried United's owner as "Bunty" since Feb 2026 — treated as the same person, an inference from role and context. "Alan Millan" appears only here.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **Asked who JVL should build a relationship with, Zazanis's first criterion was not size.** Val: "who is the master that we can build a good relationship with today?" Zazanis: "**Somebody who's honest.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **His first name was Bulldog.** "[Bulldog] is very nice" — and in the same exchange, "**Brian Deaton**. We are using five machines with him in trial. White guy. They're very nice," with Val noting he has not met them: "Not yet. I'd like to meet with them." *(The transcript renders the company "Badov" and "bed dogs"; the notes write "Bad Dog." Read as **Bulldog Gaming** — the five-machine trial matches the Bulldog trial recorded on this page for Aug 21 2026, and Brian Deaton is Bulldog's man there. An inference, labelled.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **But his one-pick was Sean Fellows.** "**If you need to choose one guy, choose this guy.**" The reason is access, not volume — detail and the identification note on [[wiki/people/sean-fellows.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **Ultra: go to the office, and the CEO is not the decision.** "Ultra also… They're a nice company. I like Ultra. I know the CEO. They're good people." But: "If you want to build a relationship with them, **the wife runs the company**… not [the CEO's] wife… **the owner's wife**… ideally, you need to go to their offices at Ultra and have him here and the owner." Val's own experience of the CEO: "**he is not committed. He says yes, but then nothing.**" A JVL voice confirmed prior contact — "You spoke to the CFO and the CEO at the show." Conflicting leadership details recorded on [[wiki/organizations/inamax-ultra.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **GLC: the operations man to know is Chris Bosnick, and he has been promoted.** Val asked about the GLC general counsel; Zazanis does not know him personally but does know the operators' side: "I know very well **Chris Bosnick** and **Mike Parham**, but they are the ones that run the show. If you want to meet somebody, **meet with Chris Bosnick. He got promoted to director.** The top guy for the [GLC], **not from the legal perspective, from the operation perspective**. He's a nice guy as long as you understand him. **He's a little bit weird the first time, but ignore the first impression.**" Val already has a route in — "Chris. I have a cell number" — and agreed with the character read. On Parham: "Mike is retired in a couple of years" (transcribed as written); the notes summarise it as "Mike Parham retiring." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **GAMOA is where the mid-sized masters are.** "There are all the [GAMOA] members. **Most of the masters, the medium, they're part of the [GAMOA]**." Named in passing and warmly: "Mike Salino… Kyle. Kyle's a nice guy." Neither is given a company here; no pages created. *(The transcript renders GAMOA as "Gamua"/"Gamboa".)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **The GLC has started auditing gift-card issuance against machine winnings.** "we receive a letter. Drop a letter at many locations requesting data… if GLC now… sees that you have a machine that has produced **ten thousand dollars of winnings**, they expect to see a similar amount [of] card issues, and some of them **they're zero**. It's a spaghetti mix. **There is no standard.**" This is the operator-side counterpart to the Georgia Lottery gift-card cash-back notice Halcyon showed Val on Aug 27 (above) — two independent sightings, four days apart, of the lottery moving on redemption data. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **ARC's own position:** "**more than 200 locations. We are number two or three, give or take**," with no cash-flow constraint ("They put money in. We have no issue with cash flow"), plus ~27 locations in **Washington DC** where it wants JVL licensed. The DC thread is on [[wiki/organizations/arc-gaming.md]]. This is the same ARC recorded in the Aug-21 pipeline above as wanting to buy its 5 trial machines and order 15 more from the first week of September. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — operator, licence and market economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Aug 31 2026 — a new sales channel: convenience-store trade shows, deliberately outside the COAM circuit

Later the same day, in a ~12-minute exchange at the JVL Georgia office (~1:54 PM ET), a colleague brought two convenience-store show dates and Val approved the channel on the spot — "**Definitely. Good thinking. Totally with you on this.**" The ruling is [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-31-convenience-store-trade-shows-as-a-channel.md]]; the stub is [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-31-c-store-trade-show-go-to-market.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_C_Store_Trade_Show_Go_To_Market-August-31-2026.md]]

- **Why it matters here:** every other Georgia motion on this page goes through the **master operators**. This one goes around them, to the **store owners** — "This is not [COAM]… The [C] store," which the notes gloss as reaching "the store owners directly rather than the operators." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_C_Store_Trade_Show_Go_To_Market-August-31-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_C_Store_Trade_Show_Go_To_Market-August-31-2026.md]]
- **The two shows:** **C-Store Connect, September 17, in Georgia** (booths possibly sold out; Val to ask a friend already exhibiting for a share of his booth) and the **GACS Southern Convenience Store Petroleum Show, October 22** — the primary target, since "it might be in time to get it." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_C_Store_Trade_Show_Go_To_Market-August-31-2026.md]]
- **A machine was already on its way to one.** "One machine is moving for a C store trade show." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_C_Store_Trade_Show_Go_To_Market-August-31-2026.md]]
- **A field figure, recorded without interpretation:** "machines that we're testing [are] performing exceptionally well," then "**Two ninety-five**" — bare, with no unit, period or machine count given in either source. It is **not** recorded here as a daily net win, and it is not reconciled against the Aug-21 hold dispute below. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_C_Store_Trade_Show_Go_To_Market-August-31-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_C_Store_Trade_Show_Go_To_Market-August-31-2026.md]]

## Aug 31 2026 — Dixie Amusement: the market numbers from a master licensee, and why he is not buying

Later the same day again (~2:31–4:00 PM ET), [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]] and [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]] visited [[wiki/people/salim-khambawala.md|Salim Khambawala]] of [[wiki/organizations/dixie-amusement.md|Dixie Amusement]] at his south-Atlanta warehouse — the **fourth Georgia operator visit in a week** — see [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-31-dixie-amusement-kiosk-wait-and-see.md]]. Dixie's own operation, the buying group and the credit-risk argument live on [[wiki/organizations/dixie-amusement.md]], [[wiki/people/salim-khambawala.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-31-dixie-defers-kiosk-investment-until-the-rules-settle.md]] and [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-31-transactional-equipment-only-with-location-exclusivity.md]]; the card-fee and float economics are on [[wiki/topics/georgia-prize-card-processor-economics.md]]. What follows is the market intelligence. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Dixie_Amusement_Kiosk_Wait_and_See_and_Market_Economics-August-31-2026.md]]

- **Val's market sizing, stated in the room and unchallenged.** "Number of machines. **Forty-seven thousand now**… and **seven thousand four hundred locations. Five point nine machine per location.** In the last three years, it was **thirty-five thousand**… So it's **twelve thousand additional machines** over three years, **four thousand more machines per year**." This is a materially bigger market than the ~45,000 machines / ~6,500 locations Felipe gave in Feb 2026 (above). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dixie_Amusement_Kiosk_Wait_and_See_and_Market_Economics-August-31-2026.md]]
- **Salim gave different equipment counts in the same breath**, and neither man reconciled them: "**Equipment was thirty eight thousand. It's now at forty four thousand.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dixie_Amusement_Kiosk_Wait_and_See_and_Market_Economics-August-31-2026.md]]
- **The "revenue is down 20–30%" claim, and Val's refusal of it.** Salim: "**All our businesses are twenty to thirty percent down** because many people aren't spending money… Gas price[s]… In June, July, normally it's down because everybody's on vacation," and separately "it's never been twenty-five percent." Val challenged it directly with the money supply: "**In the past six years, the U.S. is adding approximately six percent of money reserve on a yearly basis. That's approximately seven hundred billion dollars per year. There is a report… It's called the M two report**… **So I question whether it's truthfully, honestly, twenty-five percent down. It just doesn't make any sense**… **I've only heard that it's down here, and we're doing business elsewhere. Nobody tells me that it's down.**" *(The derived notes summarise Salim's figure as "20–25%"; the transcript says "twenty to thirty percent." Both recorded; the transcript is the primary.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dixie_Amusement_Kiosk_Wait_and_See_and_Market_Economics-August-31-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Dixie_Amusement_Kiosk_Wait_and_See_and_Market_Economics-August-31-2026.md]]
- **Salim's reframe is the durable finding: the money is up, the pot is diluted.** "Twenty five ver[sus] twenty six numbers are **four percent up, not down**… more machines, more locations, but new locations coming up. **That money is four percent above the money it was in 2025.**" His summary: "**my volume is going down. The business is getting divided, and my cost of doing business is going up.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dixie_Amusement_Kiosk_Wait_and_See_and_Market_Economics-August-31-2026.md]]
- **Replacement rate: ~20% a year now, ~10% at maturity.** Salim put masters' annual machine turnover at "**Twenty percent per year**"; Val called that "too aggressive" and said "**Normally, it's ten percent maximum.**" Salim's explanation and concession: "**currently, it's at 20% because we have to take all the legacy equipment out. We call it legacy, which is old [Pinero], [Banilla]… Aurora**," and "if everything gets updated to IGT, Aristocrat, JVL, Light and Wonder, **then it will get to 10%, as you said.**" His frame for the whole market: "**this industry is only thirteen years young.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dixie_Amusement_Kiosk_Wait_and_See_and_Market_Economics-August-31-2026.md]]
- **Price ladder, as stated.** Val: "you used to buy JVL at **fifteen thousand**. Now you're buying **Aristocrat twenty-two, twenty-three**," and of JVL today "**we're just under sixteen. So the price for you is going up.**" Salim's own history of market prices is badly garbled in the transcript; the derived notes read his last figure as **IGT ~$14,180**, and the intermediate figures are not recoverable. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dixie_Amusement_Kiosk_Wait_and_See_and_Market_Economics-August-31-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Dixie_Amusement_Kiosk_Wait_and_See_and_Market_Economics-August-31-2026.md]]
- **The four masters on the south side of Atlanta**, per Salim: himself, **Epic**, **Georgia Point** and **Paul Patel** — "Rest everybody is in **Gwinnett County**." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dixie_Amusement_Kiosk_Wait_and_See_and_Market_Economics-August-31-2026.md]]
- **The competitor field he counts: about fifteen.** "There are many, many players in the market. **I think fifteen of them that we counted who are dealing with [kiosks] and processing.**" Named in passing: "**Angela**," "**Bob Perez, which does two in one**," "**Genesis**," "**Access**." Of the German kiosk vendor Val identified as a **Gauselmann** company: "**Very overpriced**." Of **Light & Wonder**: "they just want to sell kiosks… **No monthly from the location or the master license or [InComm] or anything. But I think they are using [InComm].**" *(No pages created for Angela or Bob Perez — single passing mentions, no company attached.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dixie_Amusement_Kiosk_Wait_and_See_and_Market_Economics-August-31-2026.md]]
- **On TITO cooling.** "**a year ago, everyone was talking about TITO, and you guys came up with TITO. But then the issues I hear from TITO is service.**" JVL's line in the room: it advertised "with TITO, without TITO, no problem," always warned TITO means jams and service calls outside a casino, and "**we strongly recommend our solution, which is jamless**" — but "**the operators want to keep their dual setups.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dixie_Amusement_Kiosk_Wait_and_See_and_Market_Economics-August-31-2026.md]]
- **Access to the association is personal, not promotional.** Val asked how to get in front of Salim's ~30–40-strong buying group (about **190** in the association). Dinners were rejected outright, open houses judged tired by both men — "**People are tired of open houses**" — and Val named the only route he thought would work: Salim personally asking his friends to come for an hour. Val's reason JVL cannot draw a crowd on novelty: "**Novomatic is new for you… JVL is a known entity. We've been here for five years.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dixie_Amusement_Kiosk_Wait_and_See_and_Market_Economics-August-31-2026.md]]
- **A field event recorded nowhere else:** a JVL voice reported mid-meeting that "**All machines in Georgia are down**" that day, with the central system having failed the previous Friday too. *(Rendered "Interlock"; reading it as **Intralot** is an inference. The derived notes omit the item entirely.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dixie_Amusement_Kiosk_Wait_and_See_and_Market_Economics-August-31-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — machine counts, prices, replacement rates and operator/market economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 1 2026 — Double Down Gaming: a small volatility-driven route, and the sale that did not happen

The fifth Georgia operator visit in a week ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-01-double-down-gaming-visit.md|Sept-1 Double Down Gaming]]; [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/ryan-odonnell.md|Ryan O'Donnell]], [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]], [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe Miranda Giorgi]]) and the first to a master JVL had barely sold to. The operator profile is on [[wiki/organizations/double-down-gaming.md]]; the association intel on [[wiki/organizations/gamoa.md]]; the Class A argument on [[wiki/topics/georgia-class-a-machines.md]].

**What it adds to the Georgia market picture.** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Double_Down_Gaming_Operations_Review-September-1-2026.md]]

- **A small route, and it shrank on purpose:** ~25 locations last year, a batch sold to a partner, **12 today**, targeting **20 in six to eight months**, with **five or six locations producing 60–70% of revenue**. New locations start at **six machines**, then eight — "I don't like to go eight or nine right out of the gate."
- **JVL sits second in his brand ranking**, behind Light & Wonder and ahead of Bad Dog: "We have a handful of **Light and Wonders**. They've performed well over the last year. **The [JVLs] are kind of like our next level down.**" He has already bought six Diamond Link units.
- **His high-roller economics: $1 minimum, $10 maximum, jackpots set or reset at $12,000** — and at two of his locations the jackpot is not progressive at all. Some machines are hard-coded lower and cannot be adjusted: "**IGT doesn't allow you to set it.**"
- **The historical Georgia baseline he came from** — Primero and Banilla machines "always paying back **seventy percent to the player, sixty percent to the player**." *(Both names are garbled in the source as "primroses" and "vanillas.")*
- **Kiosk pressure rises with location quality:** "the ones that are typically the higher volume place, **the more they want kiosks**."
- **Placement is hard even for an established master:** "**I think it's hard. Locations are tough.**"

**Why no sale, and what the follow-up is.** Ryan passed — "Now I'm gonna pass right now. I've just got one place coming up… **But I always want to stay abreast**" — and wants performance numbers from his own new location before committing: "**once I get this place with some numbers, I'll see how they do.**" He would place "at least one, maybe two" JVL machines there. Val's free swap-test offer (a Diamond Link out, the new vertical in, in an established high-roller location) was blocked because his two biggest locations are partner-run. The derived notes carry the follow-up as a Val/Felipe action when the new location lands. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Double_Down_Gaming_Operations_Review-September-1-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Double_Down_Gaming_Operations_Review-September-1-2026.md]]

**The association access problem, worked a second way.** The day after asking [[wiki/people/salim-khambawala.md|Salim]] how to get in front of his buying group, Val asked a different board member for the **board-member list**, sent to him personally. See [[wiki/organizations/gamoa.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Double_Down_Gaming_Operations_Review-September-1-2026.md]]

**And the market-wide warning Val issued to a customer** — that locations will start running three validation suppliers against each other and paying none of them on time, something InComm is already reporting. On [[wiki/topics/georgia-prize-card-processor-economics.md]] and [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-31-transactional-equipment-only-with-location-exclusivity.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Double_Down_Gaming_Operations_Review-September-1-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — route size, bet limits, jackpot settings, brand mix and market economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 1 2026 (afternoon) — two more operator visits the same day: a stalled trade-in and a Texas-shaped opening

Val ran **three** Georgia operator meetings on Sept 1. After Double Down Gaming in the morning came [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-01-narayani-akshar-mega-hit-demo-and-trade-in.md|Narayani/Akshar at ~1:05 PM]] and [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-01-air-gaming-group-transactional-walkthrough.md|Air Gaming Group at ~3:00 PM]] — the sixth and seventh operator visits in seven days.

**Narayani/Akshar — the follow-up that did not close.** [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Narayani_Amusements_Mega_Hit_Demo_and_Trade_In-September-1-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Narayani_Amusements_Mega_Hit_Demo_and_Trade_In-September-1-2026.md]]

- **The principal did not come.** [[wiki/people/vishal-patel.md|Vishal Patel]] sent his operations and service managers, who cannot commit. The trade-in is re-opened between **$3,000 and $5,000** per cabinet and unsigned — [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-26-narayani-three-machine-d27-trade-in-pilot.md]].
- **A new name and a new set of numbers for the account:** the route now trades as **[[wiki/organizations/akshar-gaming.md|Akshar Gaming]]** after a change of ownership in late August, with "**close to a thousand machines**" across "**one fifty**" locations — against the ~900 machines / ~490 locations Vishal gave six days earlier for **Narayani**. The two are **not** reconciled; see the open contradiction on both org pages.
- **What the market already buys instead of JVL's suite:** a card system at ~**$5,000** one-time for six machines and **$13.30 per machine per month**, which **removes MudaGoose completely** and re-wires the location. That is a concrete competitor price point for [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]] and dated evidence for [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-31-transactional-equipment-only-with-location-exclusivity.md]].
- **Release timing, stated in the room:** "**We're going through the approval process. This will be ready for installation after we get approval**," and on a separate customer call mid-meeting, "**I cannot do it this month because we're getting approval this week. We should be okay to install Thursday and next week.**" *(The two statements are about different things — the transactional units and a software build — and neither names the approving body.)*
- **Class A:** a third operator position — not in it, considering it, already talking to **Primero**. On [[wiki/topics/georgia-class-a-machines.md]].

**Air Gaming Group — a Georgia meeting that turned into a Texas opportunity.** [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Air_Gaming_Group_Transactional_Walkthrough-September-1-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Air_Gaming_Group_Transactional_Walkthrough-September-1-2026.md]]

- An existing JVL machine customer ("**We were neighbours at one point. And we still have our machines**"; [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]]: "**They bought a lot of machines from us. Especially Altera**") sent someone for **training, not to buy** — the owner, **Karim**, decides.
- The live pain is **not Georgia**: **~100 Texas locations** whose **Big Daddy kiosks are being hacked** by copied tickets. Val's answer — the cloud as source of truth, plus a signed anti-duplication guarantee and a proposed Texas experiment — is on [[wiki/organizations/air-gaming-group.md]] and [[wiki/projects/texas-used-machine-distribution.md]].
- **Retail prices were put on the record**: Flex port ≈$1,000, Flex-IQ $1,000, kiosk ≈$5,000 (no dispenser) / ≈$12,000 (with cash dispenser), **$90 per month per location**. On [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]].
- **Follow-up agreed for Wednesday or Thursday the following week**, at Air Gaming's office — Val is back in Georgia Tuesday and leaves Friday.

**What the three Sept-1 visits have in common.** None produced an order. All three ended with a follow-up that depends on someone who was not in the room — Ryan O'Donnell's new location, Vishal Patel, and Karim. And in all three the transactional suite was pitched **alongside** whatever the operator already runs, not in place of it. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Narayani_Amusements_Mega_Hit_Demo_and_Trade_In-September-1-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Air_Gaming_Group_Transactional_Walkthrough-September-1-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Double_Down_Gaming_Operations_Review-September-1-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — route sizes, machine, device and subscription pricing and market economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 2 2026 — the eighth operator visit: the service problem named as the reason JVL is not the primary supplier

The run of Georgia operator meetings continued into a second week with [[wiki/organizations/lucky-coin.md|Lucky Coin]] at the Lawrenceville office ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-lucky-coin-service-and-upgrade-offer.md|9:29–11:09 AM ET]]; [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/kyle-shepherd.md|Kyle Shepherd]], [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]], [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe Miranda Giorgi]]). It is the first of these visits where the customer answered the question the whole programme turns on. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]]

**The finding: the blocker is service, not price, product or relationship.** Asked "what is holding you back from buying JVL as a primary supplier? Tell me the truth," the answer was "**we have more service issues out of our JVL games than we do most others**" — with specifics (a menu button that forces a re-flash, the Intralot decommission/recommission cycle turning a 15-minute fix into a two-hour one, support that "very rarely… take[s] accountability," and a refusal to help integrate third-party TITO and card readers where a competitor "will bend over backwards"). The full account is on [[wiki/organizations/lucky-coin.md]]; Val's response as doctrine is on [[wiki/topics/jvl-operating-principles.md]] (Sept 2). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]]

**What it adds to the Georgia picture:**

- **A profile of a route type the corpus had not covered:** third-generation family-owned, ~50 locations / ~250 machines, buying on cash-in and service reliability, running **EasyLink + GBT kiosks in 30–40% of locations** and PAX handhelds elsewhere, with ~15–20 JVL Flex cabinets and legacy Altairs still on route. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]]
- **The first exact machine quote plus a bundled giveaway** — $15,750 list, ~$7,000 of free transactional hardware, **$90/month per location** — on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-free-ports-and-pos-with-every-machine-bought.md]].
- **A fourth Class A operator position** ([[wiki/topics/georgia-class-a-machines.md]], Sept 2): sat it out because he distrusted the promoters' motives, now thinks the category is complementary and would place some.
- **The ATM question is what the customer actually wants to buy** — "**If it could spit out cash, I'd throw that in every one of my locations**" — and JVL cannot sell it to him; see [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-hold-the-recycler-back-but-sign-no-undertaking.md]].
- **Val's own read of the prize, to Felipe afterwards:** "I see **50 locations, of which 30 could be exclusively our equipment from the start, and you could buy 30 machines from us**." Recorded as his stated ambition, not as a forecast. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]]
- **The next step is the reverse of the usual one:** instead of the operator coming to JVL, Val goes to the route — **Wednesday September 9, 8:30 AM** at the Lucky Coin office, to hear the brother, the technicians and the route men directly. Val is back in Georgia the night of Sunday the 7th; Monday is Labor Day and Tuesday is Kyle's birthday. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]]

**And the same day, the market-level constraint was stated by Val himself to a third party.** At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-incomm-machine-payments-and-recycler-standoff.md|InComm lunch]] that followed, asked what is new on the game side of the business, he gave the answer that sits under this whole project: "**Working with masters is not easy. That's why we are trying to establish relationships at a higher level. I mean, we have many service-level relationships, but we don't have any top-level relationships… like none, zero**" — and, on why: "**it's a huge part, and there's nobody here for JVL that can do it. I don't have a senior-level management that can build relationships.**" He called it "**very problematic for us. Very concerning for me.**" The InComm side agreed the culture is the barrier — "the way deals are done… it's just a super foreign culture and system" — and that decisions get made collectively over WhatsApp with little analysis, which Val endorsed: "in our industry we say that **operators make money in spite of themselves**… there's no substance or analysis of any sort." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]]

That admission is the counterpart to the same problem recorded from the other end in February 2026 (JVL can reach only working level at United) and at Dixie on Aug 31 (no route into the buying group). The new page for the biggest of those accounts is [[wiki/organizations/united-gaming.md]], which now carries who actually signs off United's purchases.

## Sept 2 2026 (afternoon) — the ninth operator visit: an account locked to Aristocrat and Access, and Val says so to his face

The run continued the same afternoon with [[wiki/organizations/continental.md|Continental]] at Salim Punjani's own facility ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-continental-bifurcated-operations-and-vendor-lock-in.md|~3:16 PM and ~3:32 PM ET]]; [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/salim-punjani.md|Salim Punjani]], [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]], with [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]] stuck in traffic for the first half). It is the **ninth Georgia operator visit in eight days** and the first Val ended by telling the customer there is no business. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]]

**The Georgia half of this account is closed to JVL on both sides of the sale.**

- **Machines: Aristocrat only, because the locations ask for it by name.** "**In state we only are buying right now aristocrat because that's the only one that's performing**"… "**It's the only one that people want to play.**" Val probed whether the demand came from players or from the trade — "**The locations are asking.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]]
- **And Aristocrat finances it.** "**Six months, zero percent finance.**" On price, the figures in the exchange are Salim's "**I think there's one that's nineteen thousand five hundred. One is twenty thousand five hundred**", against Val's opening guess "**Twenty-five thousand**" and his later "**Eighteen thousand five hundred**." *(The derived notes compress this to "roughly $18,500–$20,500", which mixes Val's figure with Salim's two; the spoken figures are recorded here instead.)* He mixes formats: "**Both. We mix both, depending on what the customer wants.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]]
- **Card system: Access on ~75% of the estate for seven or eight years, with Novomatic taking everything else.** Detail on [[wiki/organizations/continental.md]] and [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]] (Sept 2, Continental). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Bifurcated_Operations_Texas_and_Virginia-September-2-2026.md]]
- **Georgia purchasing is stopped outright.** "**Now we're not buying anymore.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Bifurcated_Operations_Texas_and_Virginia-September-2-2026.md]]
- **Val's verdict, delivered in the room:** "**Doesn't look like we can do business here**… **It seems like your mind is set on aristocrat and access system.**" [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei]]'s in the car afterwards: "**They have a great capacity, but it looks like they're not ready to change their current partners.**" Val: "**That's absolutely correct. They have their highway.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]]

**What JVL keeps.** The relationship runs through Felipe on a fortnightly cadence — "**Filipe is always in touch with me every 15 days**… **He's the one… who got JVL back in the market**" — and Salim named his own trigger for reconsidering: "**In the future, if there's a new JVL game that comes in that people want.**" He also volunteered, unprompted, that **two JVL verticals had come back from his locations** to be replaced with Aristocrat machines; Val took it without pushing ("**I hear you. I hear you. Thank you very much.**"). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]]

**The live opportunity is out of state, not in it** — refurbished machines for Texas, five or ten at a time, on [[wiki/projects/texas-used-machine-distribution.md]] (Sept 2).

**What the ninth visit adds to the eight before it.** It is the second in two days where the customer named the specific incumbent JVL has to displace, and the first where the answer was "nobody, the incumbent is fine." Against [[wiki/organizations/lucky-coin.md|Lucky Coin]]'s morning answer — the blocker is *service* — Continental's is **performance and location demand**: locations request Aristocrat by name and nothing has come close to Banilla out of state. Neither is a price objection. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — machine prices, finance terms, route size and market economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 2 2026 (evening) — the payments side of the market, from a bank-licensed competitor

The day did not end with the ninth operator visit. [[wiki/people/billy-liu.md|Billy Liu]] of [[wiki/organizations/play-payments.md|Play Payments]] came to the Georgia office that evening ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-billy-liu-playpay-partnership-deep-dive.md|~6:14–7:45 PM ET]]) and the conversation was about the **payments layer** rather than machines. It is the first time the corpus hears the Georgia market described by someone whose licence is a banking licence, and it ended with [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]] offering to take half the company ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-val-proposes-fifty-percent-of-play-payments.md]]). [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]]

**Three market facts worth keeping, each recorded as the speaker's claim:**

- **The gaming/banking licence firewall — a structural moat nobody in the corpus had named.** "**If you have a gaming license, you won't get a banking license**… The **treasury department** will check on you… Because **traditionally they're polar opposites.**" If it holds, no COAM manufacturer or operator — JVL included — can obtain what PlayPay has, which is the whole reason the partnership conversation happened at all [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]].
- **Why Atlanta, in his account.** "**This city processes seventy percent of the world's plastic. Debit or credit, seventy percent is done here**… Nobody knows that because it's behind the scenes." He traces it to **Nova Information Systems**, a 1980s merchant-processing gateway built by his schoolfriend **Steve Hughes** that "**supplied First Union Bank. First Union became Wachovia and became Wells Fargo.**" **His assertion; uncorroborated in the corpus** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]].
- **[[wiki/organizations/gamoa.md|GAMOA]] grew out of the gift-card lobbying about fifteen years ago, and he says he helped pay for it.** "**I donated money to get GAMOA going, and I even did some lobbying on my own. That dates back 15 years.**" Full note on the association's page [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]].

**The competitor stack gains a layer.** PlayPay is already inside **EasyLink**'s redemption flow — "**Easy Links came to us and asked us, hey, would you like to provide a digital solution for us?… We built an API for them**" — so at the point of redemption an EasyLink customer chooses "**PlayPay digital wallet or gift card, physical gift card**." Billy also gestures at the kiosk maker in that stack, rendered "give and take… **Kevate kiosk**" and read here as **Gewete**, already on the competitor inventory below (an inference, not a corrected quote). A third name a JVL voice supplies — "**Batten and EasyLanes**" — is not identified and nothing is inferred from it [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]].

**And an identification worth flagging back to the Apr-21 competitor inventory.** The **"Billy Luke"** listed below as the phone-to-bank digital-wallet pitch is, on every point of description, this man. Read here as the same person and recorded as an **inference, queued for the owner** — see [[wiki/people/billy-liu.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_transactional_financial_model-April-21-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]].

**What Val offered, and what he would not give.** He put the free-hardware bundle on the table as proof of distribution — a **$16,000** machine with one internal unit, five more Flex ports and Flex-IQ thrown in, over the second SAS ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-free-ports-and-pos-with-every-machine-bought.md]]) — and argued that the only durable advantage is being first: "**There is no magic in this solution. The magic is in speed.**" What he would not commit to was the exclusivity Billy asked for (no InComm on JVL devices, no physical card dispensing): "**This is a big ask. Maybe even impossible**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** clear — licensing, market-size, fee and equity-negotiation economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 4 2026 — why V3 slipped to September 10, a live V2 defect, and the market re-measured

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-04-naming-convention-standards-and-nova-conversion.md|Sept-4 afternoon game-development meeting]] (English; Val, Michael Gottlieb, Lena Zhukova, Juan Martinez, Efim Podvoiski, Irina Shishko for part) spent its first hour reconstructing why the Georgia V3 release moved [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]].

- **The slip is attributed to the Queen of Luxor graphics update, not to bugs.** Val worked backwards, flagging his method — "I'm reading between the lines here… don't kill me" — and landed on: "the longest… duration that pushed us to September 10th is the change of graphics for Q[ueen of] Luxor." Lena confirmed it was a knowing choice: "it was a decision which we made. We knew that it will take us extra time, and **this game is ready. We can submit this product without this new graphics, but we decided to update it**." Val asked whether he had been part of that decision; Michael and Juan both said yes, with Michael clarifying "**the September 10th date wasn't discussed. It was the fact that we wanted to add Queen of Luxor**" plus post-show bug fixes. Val's closing statement of the finding: "**Felipe would have received an ISO today or Monday had it not been for Queen of Luxor.** That's the statement, that's the final statement." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **The build itself has settled.** Michael: "just in the last couple of days of this week, I have finally gotten a G[eorgia] V3 build that has settled down and is actually behaving the way that it's supposed to"; "**it's ready to submit right now. There aren't any more bugs.**" Remaining work Lena named: finishing the Queen of Luxor assets, the **expanded touch area on the nudge arrows** added the previous Friday (requiring six scenes to be re-exported, since the touch area is defined by a planar object in the 3D scene and there are six layout types), and the horse animation. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **Val accepted the Rage constraint but not the naming-convention one.** "we need to step back and acknowledge the fact that **Rage, the development environment for Rage, is not conducive to… effective output**. And for as long as we continue working on rage… the aggravation will continue. **But not not the naming convention[;] that could have been prevented… very easily.**" Root cause on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-asset-naming-convention-must-be-written-down.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **The horse animation is being redone, against Lena's report that it was approved.** Val: "The horse doesn't kick naturally… it doesn't land on… the back back legs… It needs to jump and kick, jump and kick, but it needs to land. **The physics is wrong.**" Lena: "you saw it, we approved it, it was already like released and everybody liked it" — Val: "No way. It's impossible to like." The rework is [[wiki/people/anastasia.md|Anastasiya]]'s, on **Rodeo Spins** ("Rod[eo] Spins animation is worked by Anastasiya, was also working on Queen of Luxor partially. So, she spreads herself in between the two projects"), and every other artist in the department is finishing Queen of Luxor. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **A live defect is in the field on V2.** Juan: the Jackpot Rush help text "says **win at max bet, which is not what's called for for Georgia**, and this made it out into the V2 software that's live in the field… the bug was present at the trade show, and **the 10 machines on trial for V2 have this same language** in it. The Georgia is not at max bet. Georgia is **at any bet**." No owner or fix date was set in the meeting. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **[[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]] tests a full machine before Eclipse.** Juan: "based on V2, Felipe found several problems at the show or after we got it from Eclipse. So wouldn't it be wise to get him the ISO build and test the full machine with Topper and everything before we send it to Eclipse? That's an extra day or two." Val accepted the cost. Michael thought Felipe already had an ISO; Juan said he did not. Val's schedule arithmetic in the same passage: September 10, "give or take another day or so," then "**Five days for… Eclipse, so we're now somewhere in closer to the 20th. September 20th release.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **The Georgia skill initiative stays on hold.** Michael asked where it stands; Val: "it's on hold. I wanted to understand… what may come out from it, but it's still on hold. I need to understand if we will have a customer… **commitment to purchase. And I still do not have it. But next week I'm meeting with the CEO.**" Michael and Efim still need more competitor game footage — free spins, racking and hot streak, at the highest bet and **not** on autoplay — to resolve maths questions; Val undertook to record it. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **The market, re-measured on Val's own tour.** "**it's 47,000 machines in 7,400 locations. with 5.9 machines per location.** Two years ago, it was **35,000 machines in 6,200 locations**. And when I started Georgia Market, it was **25,000 machines in 5,000 locations**. That was **five years ago**." His character sketch of the channel — C-stores, gas stations, variety stores, "dirty locations for the most part, poor service, machines are usually locked up in a small room or in between the boxes and cases," "run by Indians and Pakistanis," "a Uber super duper Uber gossipy environment" where advertising works by word of mouth — is the reasoning behind the premium-cabinet play on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-d27-discontinued-curve-c43-premium-line.md]]. *(Note these figures sit alongside, and do not reconcile with, the ~45,000 machines / ~6,500 locations Felipe gave in February 2026 — different sources, different dates, both recorded.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **Cabinet lineup for this market:** D27 discontinued with no upgrades offered; V43 continues through versions 4, 5 and 6 for about three years; **C43 Curve** shown at the next trade show, developed in NOVA. The V4 interface package (mechanical button deck, bet up/down defaulting to $1, dual box in place of nudge, 14–15 games on the selection screen) is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-georgia-v4-mechanical-button-deck.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **How the $15,750 price and the free-hardware package actually came about** — Val's own account, given here for the first time — is on [[wiki/topics/jvl-machine-pricing-strategy.md]] (Sept-4). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **§15.8:** clear — product, schedule and market economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 4 2026 (midday) — the LTE modem will not hold a link in Georgia: same model number, different source, different firmware

Three and a half weeks after the [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-10-lte-modem-v1000-georgia-coam.md|Aug-10 V-1000 session]] closed with "test it on real Georgia machines with a real modem, because the same vendor's modem for different markets can be incompatible," that is exactly what happened. On the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-04-lte-modem-firmware-mismatch-and-kore-settings.md|Sept-4 midday call]] (Russian, 12:10–12:29 PM ET; [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]], [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi Peari]]) Pavel reported that the modem fitted in Georgia — a "**7455**" — does not work, after two days of experiments with [[wiki/people/elizabeth.md|Elizabeth Palomino]] failed to configure a stable link [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_LTE_Modem_Firmware_Mismatch_and_KORE_Settings-September-4-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_LTE_Modem_Firmware_Mismatch_and_KORE_Settings-September-4-2026.md]].

- **The diagnosis:** Minsk's test unit came from AliExpress, Georgia's same-model-number unit came from a different source, and the firmware versions differ — Genadi's "ошибка номер один." Pavel rejected the "mistake" framing and noted the Minsk test was run two weeks earlier; nothing on the call reconciles the two readings. Full detail, and the two remedies set running in parallel — buy the ~$16 North-America variant, and ask **KORE** both which modems it recommends and for the modem configuration parameters including the **APN** — on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-north-america-modem-variant-and-ask-kore-for-the-apn.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_LTE_Modem_Firmware_Mismatch_and_KORE_Settings-September-4-2026.md]].
- **Antenna mounting moves, but does not settle.** The Aug-10 open question below now has field evidence: insufficient antenna signal was one cause of the failure to connect; **two antennas are now fitted to the module**; and Genadi wants the antenna placed into an opening rather than glued flat to the metal enclosure, because "все сигналы же уходят." No final mounting solution was chosen [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_LTE_Modem_Firmware_Mismatch_and_KORE_Settings-September-4-2026.md]].
- **The schedule has slack.** Val: there is time, because the graphics and another item (garbled) are not ready either — which matches the V3 slip to September 10 recorded in the afternoon meeting above [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_LTE_Modem_Firmware_Mismatch_and_KORE_Settings-September-4-2026.md]].
- **Commercially this is the modem the whole data programme rides on** — [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-21-cell-modem-on-every-machine-from-september-1.md]] and [[wiki/topics/machine-data-repurchase-program.md]]. As of Sept 4 the fleet-wide fitment ruling is unchanged; what is not yet true in the field is that a fitted modem actually connects [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_LTE_Modem_Firmware_Mismatch_and_KORE_Settings-September-4-2026.md]].
- **§15.8:** clear — component pricing and vendor economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 7 2026 — the convenience-store show becomes a product deadline, and Val pushes installations ahead of it

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-07-gacs-show-setup-flex-iq-flex-ports-and-the-e-wallet.md|Sept 7 2026 8:00 AM call]] (Russian) is where the trade-show channel approved on Aug 31 turns into an engineering schedule. [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]] opened it with "У нас следующая выставка[ —] октябрь 21," and the derived notes name the show **GACS** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]. **The date does not match the one recorded on Aug 31 — see the open contradiction below.**

- **The stand is a sales configuration, not an exhibit.** Val, interrupting the agenda: "мы сейчас не просто говорим, что вот у нас будет трейд[-шоу]… Мы сейчас активно занимаемся продажей… определённых решений в Джорджии. Одн[о] из основных решений — это флекс[-]айкью и порты. И флекспей." Klimovich agreed and called the show "наш конкретный milestone с конкретной датой[,] дедлайном," with the stand built on "текущем нашем понимании требовани[й] рынка." Three setups are planned — Flex-IQ with Flex-Ports, the **Pay Slim** kiosk, and the Flex recycler; the device detail is on [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]] (Sept 7, 8:00) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].
- **Val will not let the show set the pace.** "Когда мы говорим про выставку, мы автоматически отбегаем это на октябрь месяц[,] на конец октября. У нас… я работаю сильно над тем, чтобы сделать **installation в сентябре**. Сейчас. **Мне нужен продукт сейчас**… тот, кто захватит сегодня вот этот рынок [первым,] тот будет им контролировать. То есть сегодня нужно работать в системе **over[-]драйв**[.] Вот эта система регулярной… планированной работы[ —] её надо чуть-чуть запарковать… Мне нужн[ы] IQ [и] flex pay. **Завтра**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].
- **Two Georgia market facts stated in passing.** Room size: "лак[ейшн]ы очень маленькие, там **в среднем стоит 6 машин**" — consistent with the 5.9-machines-per-location arithmetic Val gave operators on Aug 31 and Sept 4 above. And the incumbent redemption habit JVL's configurable wallet lifetime is being set against: "обычно локации[,] всё, что я видел… здесь, в Джорджии[ —] они дают **30 дней**[,] срок жизни тито[-]тикета" (Klimovich) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].
- **What this costs JVL commercially in this market:** installing now means displacing the incumbent's system outright, not sitting beside it — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-flex-iq-cannibalises-mudagoose-no-parallel-operation.md]], which hardens the exclusivity term of [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-31-transactional-equipment-only-with-location-exclusivity.md]] into a product constraint and has JVL running its own cabling or its own wireless network at each location [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].
- **§15.8:** clear — market, show and product economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 8 2026 — two operator visits in one day: how thin the transactional market actually is, and how the market is structured

**Morning, [[wiki/organizations/arc-gaming.md|ARC Gaming]]** ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-08-arc-gaming-bundle-sanity-check-and-david-sloan-intro.md|11:09–11:18 AM]]). Two penetration figures went into the room and **they are the lowest in the corpus**: [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Vertical_Screen_Bundle_Offer_and_David_Intro-September-8-2026.md]]

- **[[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]]: "At least seventy percent of locations still operating with the [PAX] system."** He used it as the reason to move now — "a lot of locations are undecided… I would like to have an early jump on the locations, and I would like to get operators' attention." *(The transcript writes "PACS system"; the derived notes read it as **PAX**, noting the Aug-31 ARC record of ~90 PAX locations. The reading is the notes'.)* [[raw/Meeting_Notes_ARC_Gaming_Vertical_Screen_Bundle_Offer_and_David_Intro-September-8-2026.md]]
- **[[wiki/people/georgios-zazanis.md|George Zazanis]]: under ten percent of the market uses any transactional equipment at all, from any vendor.** "If you go to [InComm] and if you have [InComm] to give you data overall for the Georgia market, they would say **not even let's say ten percent of the market is using any kind of equipment**… **Any kind doesn't matter from where they are.** So the market is still a cash market, even if we like it or we don't like it."
- **The two do not obviously agree** — 70 % of locations on PAX versus under 10 % of the market using any equipment — and neither man addressed the other's figure. *(Val's is about **locations** and Zazanis's about "the market"; whether that difference explains the gap is not stated in the source, and no reconciliation is attempted here.)* Zazanis's own counterweight: "**at the same time there is room for growth. The market is very brand new to any kind of equipment.**" Compare the Sept-4 internal figure already on this page — "only 20 % of locations have some sort of system." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Vertical_Screen_Bundle_Offer_and_David_Intro-September-8-2026.md]]
- **And Zazanis's assessment of the competitive noise:** "**the last three, four weeks… I have seen at least so many different solutions from from everybody. You have no idea**… it's a mess… **it's the right time to put one more thing on top of everything else.**"

**Afternoon, [[wiki/organizations/dixie-amusement.md|Dixie Amusement]]** ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-08-dixie-amusement-bundle-offer-and-card-economics.md|~3:00 PM]]). [[wiki/people/salim-khambawala.md|Salim Khambawala]] supplied the market's structure rather than its size: [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dixie_Amusement_Georgia_Transactional_Equipment_and_Bundle_Economics-September-8-2026.md]]

- **190 licences, ~80 real operators, and a 20/40 split inside his own orbit.** "there is only **190 master licenses** out there, but **only 80 operators** out there"; "from even that **sixty**, I would say **twenty would talk what we are talking. The rest forty is monkey see, monkey do** type of mindset." Val: "It's always the **eighty-twenty rule** in everything." *(Compare his Aug-31 figures above: 190 in the association, his own buying group ~30–40.)*
- **Georgia regulates three tiers and only three.** "**In Georgia, the regulation is only on three sectors. One is a distributor, JVL. One is a master, and one is a location.**" Which is why a distributor cannot deal with the location directly: "**It's the master that negotiates for the location. It's not the distributor that negotiates**" (Val), and Salim's contrast with other markets — "in some of those countries, like Colombia or anywhere in the U.S., **it's the distributor who's directly installing at the casino**. So that **middleman** is now pushing the envelope."
- **A location may not take more than half its gross from COAM.** "**in the law it says you cannot [derive] more than fifty percent of your gross revenue of the store from these [COAM]**… **You cannot open a game room and put 50 games in there.**"
- **Servicing is the master's job by statute**, which bounds who can touch a JVL device in the field: "**Any repair or maintenance in the equipment has to be master licensed**… I think in the law it was written in **twenty seventeen, two thousand and fourteen**." Full treatment on [[wiki/people/salim-khambawala.md]].
- **The machine-count figure holds.** Val restated "**forty-seven thousand machines in the market**" with **~10 % annual replacement ≈ 4,000 machines a year** — the same numbers used on Aug 31 and Sept 4 above — and added a **state average of $108 per machine**. The single-SAS sizing built on them is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-flex-port-goes-sas-and-jvl-drops-the-old-machine-segment.md]].
- **Who pays the monthly connection fee is still being fought over**, and both men expect it to land on the master: "**it's just a push of a shield for negotiation for now**" (Salim); "**eventually, master will pay because master will buy equipment**" (Val). Detail on [[wiki/organizations/gamoa.md]].

**§15.8:** clear — market penetration, structure, licence counts and machine economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 9 2026 — a working prototype in Georgia by the 15th, and the Eclipse submission restated for the same date

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-09-september-resource-plan-and-flex-port-readiness.md|Sept-9 7:02 AM call]] [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]] set two Georgia dates inside one minute:

- **A working Flex-Port prototype in Georgia on September 15.** "**15 сентября[,] в Джорджии должен быть работающий прототип**" — with the Flex-Port connecting over **SAS 2** on machines from other suppliers as well as JVL's own: "флег[с]пор[т] должен подключаться к сас 2… на разных машинах, то есть мы должны взять машины у других [саплаеров]." [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]] wants that split into two tracked items and expects third-party machines to break immediately. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-negative-cases-first-and-flex-ports-tested-in-borrowed-non-jvl-machines.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]].
- **The Eclipse submission around the same date, which forces the V3 testing.** Val: the submission has to be handed in "где[-]то 15 числа[.] Это значит, что **[V]3 продукт должен быть досконально протестирован**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]]. *(The transcript renders the recipient garbled — "опру с клипса", "значимый клип"; the derived notes read it as the Eclipse submission [[raw/Meeting_Notes_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]], and that reading matches the Sept-15 Eclipse slip already recorded on [[wiki/topics/nova-platform-architecture.md]]. Recorded on the notes' authority, with the garble flagged.)*
- **And the game-maths change that has to land inside that window** — credits instead of currency on the buttons, plus new maths on two games, due to the team by Sept 11: [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-08-credits-not-currency-on-the-buttons-and-two-games-remathed.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]].
- **GCOAM V3 testing is still unfinished**, which is why [[wiki/people/alexei-khamin.md|Khamin]] cannot yet be moved onto the transactional work: "есть незавершённые работы по тестированию там [ГК]ам в 3" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]].

**Neither Sept-9 source mentions the GACS convenience-store show or its date**, so the open contradiction below is untouched by this batch.

## Sept 9 2026 (afternoon) — two more operator visits: the installer's view of the market, and a route that measures JVL by its service calls

The same day the engineering dates above were set, Val ran two counterparty meetings: [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-09-rao-machine-performance-and-exclusivity-offer.md|Rao at 10:04 AM]] and [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-09-lucky-coin-service-burden-and-exclusive-partnership.md|Lucky Coin at 1:31 PM]]. Together they are the **ninth and tenth operator/distributor visits** in this run.

### The installer's numbers — the first view of the Georgia transactional market from the people who physically install it

[[wiki/people/narasimha-rao-namburi.md|Rao]] sells and installs **Access**, and his figures are the only installation-side data in the corpus [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rao_Machine_Performance_and_Exclusivity_Offer-September-9-2026.md]]:

- **350 locations converted to Access in about three to three and a half months**, three to four crews, three installs a day each; **thirty minutes per machine**, "**at least three hours**" for a six-machine location; about **$2,500 per install** on equipment resale. Full detail on his page.
- **The recurring side does not work for him at all.** Masters do not pay the monthly fee, locations never pay, and "**mostly, I'm losing a little bit right now because of subscription.**" That is a direct field datum on the recurring-revenue premise underneath [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-31-transactional-equipment-only-with-location-exclusivity.md]].
- **5,500 locations on PAX with [[wiki/organizations/incom.md|InComm]], "all of it needs to be replaced"** (Val's figure, in the room). **This is a different quantity from the penetration numbers already on this page** — Val's Sept-8 "at least seventy percent of locations still operating with the PAX system" and Zazanis's "not even ten percent of the market is using any kind of equipment." **None of the three is reconciled with the others.** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Vertical_Screen_Bundle_Offer_and_David_Intro-September-8-2026.md]]
- **And the targeting rule Val drew from it:** "**You're not reinstalling equipment into the other system. You're only going into location where there is no system. Right now is the time to catch it.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rao_Machine_Performance_and_Exclusivity_Offer-September-9-2026.md]]
- **Val's argument for why every location is a fresh sale** — the clearest statement of it in the corpus: "**It's always new per location**… It's not a casino… **Each location of six machines goes through a discovery cycle, through an uncomfortable cycle, through an introductory cycle, through a friendship cycle.
