Every single six machines, it is a new thing. It doesn't matter if [the] location across the street had it**… each variety store [has] their own clientele." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rao_Machine_Performance_and_Exclusivity_Offer-September-9-2026.md]]
- **The saturation warning, from a man who put 350 locations in this year:** "it would have come here like **two years ago**. It would have been nice. **It's already almost saturated. If we start now, by the time we pick up, it's saturated.**" Val agreed on timing, declined the conclusion, and pointed at **Texas** — "**Texas, for example, is booming**" — adding that he has "**a building in Austin… with a vacant unit**" where an office could open. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rao_Machine_Performance_and_Exclusivity_Offer-September-9-2026.md]]
- **Rao's own "200 locations lined up" for Access turn out not to be new business** — "**Those masters have access already previously**" — which is the route-consolidation pattern already on this page seen from the installer's side. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rao_Machine_Performance_and_Exclusivity_Offer-September-9-2026.md]]
- **InComm is expected to welcome JVL's hardware, per Val:** "**[InComm] will happily do it because they don't want to be involved in it. They want somebody like us to come in and put our equipment… They want to clear cards. They don't want to bother with equipment.**" Consistent with what InComm has said about itself since 2025; see [[wiki/organizations/incom.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rao_Machine_Performance_and_Exclusivity_Offer-September-9-2026.md]]
- **A sales lead and a market contact:** JVL is "**taking… 65 machines on trad[e]**" from an operator (Rao could not use them — he sells only in Georgia); and Val asked for a direct line to **David Milani** (also said as "Vilari"), a younger **Bad Dog** salesman distinguished from an older "Greg" — Rao's warning: "**very close to [Access]**." *(The surrounding employment history is garbled — "Bandel", "Raikon security" — and nothing is inferred from it. "Bad Dog" remains unresolved to any corpus entity.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rao_Machine_Performance_and_Exclusivity_Offer-September-9-2026.md]]
- **Val also flagged Puerto Rico as a topic to take up with him:** "**I'd like to speak to you about Puerto Rico.**" No content followed. See [[wiki/projects/puerto-rico-expansion.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rao_Machine_Performance_and_Exclusivity_Offer-September-9-2026.md]]

### The September-2 letter, read aloud and dismissed

Val raised an **email dated September 2** that he had read and did not follow — "**I read that email and I don't understand it**" — and a JVL colleague summarised its position: [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rao_Machine_Performance_and_Exclusivity_Offer-September-9-2026.md]]

> "on one side they say… **we did not [want] players to get close to the cash so quickly and slowly, so we expected that when they will get this card, in most cases they will keep the card. They will never go to cash. I know how stupid it sounds, but that's the message. And now we see that technology is already providing a solution where you can get the cash immediately, and we are worried about that. That's not what we wanted.**"

- **Val's reaction:** "**this is not even a legislator. This is some lawyer who dreamt up limitations of it, without considering technology**"; "**they can't control it**"; and the underlying point he has made repeatedly — "**you cannot stop the player because the location will be the ATM**… **The player will wager cash and will walk away with cash. And you gotta be dumb if you don't understand it.**"
- **Asked directly whether JVL should change how it enters the market, he said no.** The colleague's question — "**Should we be worried about that? Should we change the approach [to] how we enter the markets?**" — Val: "**No, I don't think so.**"
- **What he did accept as possible** is a collective operator boycott: "operators could get together, and… say, **we collectively decide not to promote the system in our locations**… **we are not going to buy it**… **But I'm fine with that. What does it have to do with the technology?**" See [[wiki/organizations/gamoa.md]].
- **The author is not established.** The passage ends with a name spoken as "**I'm Chad DePietro**" in the middle of the read-out; **no such person appears anywhere else in the corpus**, the line may be a Plaud artefact, and **nothing is inferred from it**. Whether this letter is the same document as the **Aug-27 gift-card cash-back notice** or the **Aug-31 GLC data letter** already on this page is **not stated in any source** and is not asserted here. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Rao_Machine_Performance_and_Exclusivity_Offer-September-9-2026.md]]

### The route that measures JVL by its service calls

The afternoon visit to [[wiki/organizations/lucky-coin.md|Lucky Coin]] is the fullest field account of JVL machine reliability in the corpus — decommission/recommission, a lottery-disabled game with $2,600 of player credits on it, V-deck spills, and the technicians' verdict that JVL takes "**above average**" attention while the games themselves "**do well enough**." It sits on the organization page. What it adds to the Georgia picture here is the **buyer-side price band** — JVL at $15,750 against a new Aristocrat at **$18,000–19,000** and a new Bad Dog game at **$10,000–12,000** ([[wiki/topics/jvl-machine-pricing-strategy.md]]) — and the fact that the largest exclusivity ask JVL has made in this market is now sitting with one operator, answer due **Sept 15 2026** ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-val-asks-lucky-coin-for-its-entire-business.md]]). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_Burden_and_Exclusive_Partnership-September-9-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — installation, route, market-size and machine economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 9 2026 (evening) — the eleventh counterparty visit, and the day's own summing-up

The same day closed with two more recordings: the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-09-arc-gaming-backend-displacement-negotiation.md|4:35 PM meeting with ARC's CEO]] — the **eleventh** operator/distributor counterparty in this run, after the ninth and tenth that morning and afternoon — and the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-09-georgia-exclusivity-bet-and-sebastian-reassignment.md|5:31 PM internal debrief]] an hour later. **Six recordings in one day.** [[raw/Meeting_Notes_ARC_Gaming_Backend_Displacement_Negotiation-September-9-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Georgia_Exclusivity_Bet_and_Sebastian_Reassignment-September-9-2026.md]]

### What the ARC meeting adds to the market picture

- **The strategy behind the whole run is stated out loud to a customer for the first time.** The free hardware converts the location; the **back end** is the object. "we're virtually converting the entire location to us. **On the back end, we're looking to replace [InComm]**" — said with no confidentiality agreement in place. [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-val-discloses-the-incomm-displacement-intent-to-arcs-ceo.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Backend_Displacement_Negotiation-September-9-2026.md]]
- **A master operator's CEO sizes the back-end prize himself** — 47,000 machines, five cash-outs each a day, eighty per cent control, "**It's a million dollars**" — and calls it "a great business model." Figures and the arithmetic caveats on [[wiki/topics/georgia-prize-card-processor-economics.md]]. His 47,000 matches Val's own Sept-4 re-measurement recorded above. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Backend_Displacement_Negotiation-September-9-2026.md]]
- **And the same CEO prices what a player loses cashing a card** — $2 card, $3 ATM fee plus $2.50 — and says he would give up his own cut to undercut the next operator: "**I'll give up three dollars to gain twenty dollars.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Backend_Displacement_Negotiation-September-9-2026.md]]
- **Class A, from the operator running it: "It's not doing great."** Machines are being placed opportunistically — "**We're just slinging them out and seeing what sticks**" — and the licence, not the economics, is why ARC is in the category. Full account on [[wiki/topics/georgia-class-a-machines.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Backend_Displacement_Negotiation-September-9-2026.md]]
- **The whole-account ask failed here too.** "**I don't think you can buy my entire business**" — ARC already runs several point-of-sale systems besides its kiosk vendor's. [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-arc-account-exclusivity-sought-and-not-obtained.md]]. **So both account-level exclusivity asks made on September 9 are now unanswered or refused** — Lucky Coin's answer is due Sept 15; ARC's CEO said no on the spot. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Backend_Displacement_Negotiation-September-9-2026.md]]
- **Next: Wednesday September 16 2026, at Sloan's place.** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Backend_Displacement_Negotiation-September-9-2026.md]]

### The debrief

- **Val's read of the market, in one line:** "**the market is literally bottomless.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Exclusivity_Bet_and_Sebastian_Reassignment-September-9-2026.md]]
- **A name resolved.** **"Lucky Bucks" is ARC's former name** — one company, renamed — and **ARC is the state's number two operator**: both are Val's rulings of 2026-09-10, recorded in the derived notes. Felipe's Feb-2026 master map above already carried the pair as one entry, "Lucky Bucks / Arc Luck (~350 locations)". **The corpus's many earlier Lucky Bucks mentions have not been retro-edited**; see the name note on [[wiki/organizations/arc-gaming.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Georgia_Exclusivity_Bet_and_Sebastian_Reassignment-September-9-2026.md]] [[raw/To-Do_Georgia_meetings-February-18-2026.md]]
- **A colleague's doubt about the Class A supplier bet**, and Val's answer that others "will be in it" with no timing — on [[wiki/topics/georgia-class-a-machines.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Exclusivity_Bet_and_Sebastian_Reassignment-September-9-2026.md]]
- **Field staffing changed for the rollout:** [[wiki/people/sebastian.md|Sebastian]] takes an assignment off [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]], must arrive before the end of September, and is to be paired with an unnamed local quasi-distributor for site access — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-sebastian-replaces-felipe-on-the-georgia-field-assignment.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Exclusivity_Bet_and_Sebastian_Reassignment-September-9-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — operator, card, licence and market economics plus a field assignment; **no named-person compensation recorded.**

## Sept 10 2026 — JVL introduces itself to the regulator

The twelfth Georgia counterparty meeting in as many days, and the first with the **state** rather than the market: [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]] alone in the COAM Hearing Room at the [[wiki/organizations/georgia-lottery-corporation.md|Georgia Lottery Corporation]], 10:21–11:20 AM ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-10-georgia-lottery-coam-manufacturer-introduction.md]]). Everything the wiki previously held about the Lottery's position came second-hand through [[wiki/organizations/incom.md|InComm]] or through operators; this is first-hand [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Georgia_Lottery_Class_A_Guidance_and_Prize_Card_Compliance-September-10-2026.md]].

What it produced for this project:

- **The prize-card rule, stated by the regulator.** The gift card cannot be exchanged for currency **at the location where the prize is won**; what the holder does with it elsewhere is treated as outside the statute. Full text and the "three fifty-eight nine" statute reference on [[wiki/topics/georgia-prize-card-processor-economics.md]] (Sept 10) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Lottery_Class_A_Guidance_and_Prize_Card_Compliance-September-10-2026.md]].
- **Class A manufacturing is lawful today**, with no rulebook, no central system and no certification — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-10-the-lottery-tells-jvl-class-a-manufacturing-is-lawful-today.md]]. Val's own response was to wait for the January legislature — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-10-no-class-a-build-until-the-january-legislature-is-heard.md]].
- **A venue for the regulator to inspect JVL's transactional equipment:** Intralot's lab, not the Lottery's own — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-10-the-transactional-equipment-demonstration-goes-to-intralot.md]]. The Lottery also asked for an **updated JVL cabinet** to replace the older one it holds [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Lottery_Class_A_Guidance_and_Prize_Card_Compliance-September-10-2026.md]].
- **A route to [[wiki/people/joe-kim.md|Joe Kim]]**, whom Val has approached three times through a general inquiry address and never reached; the staff undertook to supply a direct phone number and email [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Lottery_Class_A_Guidance_and_Prize_Card_Compliance-September-10-2026.md]].
- **JVL's stated market position, on the record with the regulator:** "**I'm not going after A customers. I'm going after B customers**", because a Class B customer is licensed and "**they have a lot to lose**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Lottery_Class_A_Guidance_and_Prize_Card_Compliance-September-10-2026.md]].
- **Two spend signals:** JVL is not exhibiting at G2E ("**I can't afford it**"), and the Lottery missed JVL's booth at the Southern Amusement Expo and apologised for it [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Lottery_Class_A_Guidance_and_Prize_Card_Compliance-September-10-2026.md]].

## Sept 10 2026 (afternoon) — the thirteenth counterparty visit, and the first committed machine order of the run

Straight from the Lottery to [[wiki/organizations/diamond-amusement.md|Diamond Amusement]]'s warehouse in Conyers, 12:29–2:28 PM ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-10-diamond-amusements-machine-performance-and-bundle-pilot.md]]) — the longest operator visit of the three-week run and the only one to end with a number. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]

- **The first committed machine order since the show: two.** Val asked Diamond to trade its whole **74-machine** JVL fleet for the new vertical cabinet and was refused — "**I'm not going to upgrade 74 games**" — then talked the customer *down* from a ten-machine counter-offer: "**Don't do ten. Do two.**" Result: **two machines, two different location types, targeting October 1** — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-10-diamond-buys-two-machines-instead-of-trading-the-fleet.md]]. Against the run's other asks (Rao "I'm in" with no quantity; Lucky Coin answering Sept 15; ARC's CEO refusing), this is the only committed order. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]
- **The bundle put to a tenth counterparty**, with the $90/month back after three Sept-9 meetings omitted it, the free set priced at **$15,000 retail**, the machine quoted at **both $15,750 and $16,000** in one conversation, and — new — **the API free while "operator intelligence" becomes chargeable later**. Compositions on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-free-ports-and-pos-with-every-machine-bought.md]]; prices on [[wiki/topics/jvl-machine-pricing-strategy.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]
- **The exclusivity condition stated flat, and the enforcement half refused by a second master licensee.** "**we want exclusivity in the location. Nothing else can operate there. As far as transactional**" — and on the money, "**I'm not going to be a reseller**… **it's better for us to have an arms-length relationship**", with the same lottery objection to a remote shut-off that InComm and Salim gave. [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-31-transactional-equipment-only-with-location-exclusivity.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-10-diamond-will-not-guarantee-card-clearance-debt.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]
- **Half an hour of service complaints — the sharpest customer-side account of JVL's Georgia product problems in the corpus** (logic-box height, monitor remounting, the settings/passcode lock, unnamed peripheral faults, error 704, heat, failed field updates, re-commissioning, **five JVL machines down** on Diamond's Intralot sheet). Val conceded all of it, promised a year of step-by-step fixes **after** the new game ships, and refused only the logic-box move. Full list on [[wiki/organizations/diamond-amusement.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]
- **The Georgia data problem, named and partly solved.** Val rejected Diamond's Intralot-derived financials to its face ("**[Intralot] generates erroneous reports**… payouts of a million dollars") and got the operator's **field auditor** to start capturing settings data off JVL machines on his compliance rounds — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-10-diamond-field-auditor-captures-jvl-settings-data.md]]. His own words for the gap: "**we're operating blind.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]
- **A settings explanation for why JVL ranks behind the big three in Georgia**, with numbers, for the first time: default bet **$1.00 vs $0.25**, RTP **90% vs 92–94%**, displayed top progressive **$6,000 vs $2,400** — on [[wiki/topics/slot-game-design-principles.md]] (Sept 10). Unproven. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]
- **The morning's regulatory answer relayed to the market within two hours**, and correctly summarised back by the operator — "**So Justin and Chris said it's okay, just not in the same licensed facility.**" See [[wiki/topics/georgia-prize-card-processor-economics.md]] (Sept 10) and [[wiki/people/joe-kim.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]
- **Service capacity: an engineer from Miami joins the Georgia service load on October 1**, taking work off [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]] — unnamed in the source; the Sebastian reading is a labelled inference on [[wiki/people/sebastian.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]
- **Three follow-ups inside the week:** a location tour with JVL's senior VP of game design (targeting Tuesday Sept 15), the customer visiting JVL's office **Wednesday Sept 16 at about 3:00 PM** to see the transactional system, and dinner that evening. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]
- **§15.8:** clear — machine, fleet, bundle and operator economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 11 2026 — the mixed-machine compatibility window, and the Georgia office as rehearsal stage

Two things about Georgia came out of the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-11-transactional-mvp1-scope-and-priorities.md|Sept-11 8:04 AM engineering call]], and both are about the physical market rather than the product.

**Flex-Port compatibility with other manufacturers' cabinets is the live MVP-1 risk, and the Georgia office cannot test it.** [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]] flagged that Flex-Port compatibility will be implemented for some machine models and not others, may need different logic per type, and may need per-cabinet work landing even after MVP 2. [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]] agreed they hit it in MVP 1 already, and answered Pavel's direct question — are there any non-JVL cabinets in the Georgia office? — with "[з]десь нет". His plan [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]:

> "мы договариваемся сейчас с некоторыми мастерами, что или мы к ним приедем в [вэр]хаус, где есть много разных машин, или они дадут нам на день[-]2 сюда к нам в [вэр]хаус своих несколько машин. Но к этому моменту у нас должно быть всё условно готово для тестирования. Потому что это речь идёт о 1[-]2 днях максимум."

The session itself: fit a Flex-Port, run a quick compatibility test, get a problem or an OK, then "переставляем этот флекс[]порт… на максимальном количестве машин за короткое время и собираем информацию." *(The transcript's word is "мастерами" — master licensees — not operators; recorded as spoken.)* **Everything must be ready for testing by then, and the window is one to two days maximum.**

**The first installation is a real, mixed-manufacturer location.** Klimovich: "мы устанавливаемся на реальную локацию[;] там будет… как минимум одна машина [JVL,] но и машины других производителей" *(the transcript renders the manufacturer as "GL")* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]].

**The network dependency this creates.** Pavel: the team needs the **VPN into the Georgia office** by then so it can work directly from the internal network — "нам к этому времени 100% нужно… VPN в Джорджия офис, чтоб мы могли из нашей внутренней сети… работать напрямую" — and asked how that works off-site at a guest warehouse. [[wiki/people/andrey-bosik.md|Andrey Bosik]]'s answer was a **router with an LTE modem**, which Pavel turned into a requirement — "нам нужно… такое требование, что у нас маршрутизатор[…] с LT[E] модемом… [и] настроен." Bosik went to check whether the router he had already requested carries one. Klimovich: "это хороший вопрос, его надо очень быстро решать" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]].

**The Georgia office is also the rehearsal stage for October 1.** It already holds assembled Flex IQ units, so the rehearsal is installing the new software, connecting the TITO printer and moving the QR scanner — a job [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi]] should control — with [[wiki/people/elizabeth.md|Elizabeth]] able to flash Flex-Ports if needed. Detail on [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]] (Sept 11) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]].

**A typical Georgia location, described for the Wi-Fi work:** a petrol station with four machines by the wall in the retail area and another five around a corner behind a wall, all counted as **one location**, about **five metres** between points, with walls in between [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]].

**Games in Georgia: six today, ten or fifteen contemplated.** At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-11-nova-game-change-regulatory-procedure.md|9:30 call]] [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan Martinez]] described the standing position — "**we always submitted six games**", with [[wiki/people/elizabeth.md|Elizabeth]] or [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]] free to enable only four — and the change now on the table, "**going from six games to the 10 or 15 that we're looking at now for Georgia**", which *does* require telling the Lottery. See [[wiki/organizations/georgia-lottery-corporation.md]] (Sept 11) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_NOVA_Game_Change_Regulatory_Procedure-September-11-2026.md]].

## Sept 11 2026 (11:07) — the coming guidance is relayed outside JVL, with a draft said to be on the way

Val gave an outside payment processor — [[wiki/organizations/digital-cash-processing.md|Digital Cash Processing / ICP Inc.]], at the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-11-digital-cash-processing-card-solution-and-cash-float-partnership.md|11:07 AM call]] — the reading he had received from the Lottery in person the day before. **This is the first time the "same location" rule leaves JVL, and it carries one thing the Sept-10 record does not: that a draft is coming.** [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]]

> "**The latest, the latest regulatory ruling that will be coming out in Georgia is that a transaction from a card onto an ATM machine that is in the same location is illegal. Okay. And… I just want to point out that the wording is in the same location.**"
> — asked whether it is written yet: "**Apparently, they're coming out with the… draft.**"
> [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]]

- **Why the state is panicking, in his words.** "**Every location today has an ATM machine. And what Georgia does not have and does not allow is gambling. The machines are not gambling machines; they're skill machines. The skill machines do not award cash… If you are promoting the path from award to cash, this is exactly what Georgia does not want and does not have.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]]
- **He named JVL's own show stand as the thing that drew it,** more explicitly than in any earlier source: "**We showed a hardware device that takes cash, then issues a ticket or a card. You could walk around the machines. You could play machines, and you could go back into the same hardware device and receive cash as though it's an ATM machine. So we showed that device on our stand. And the… general counsel from [the Georgia Lottery] had a negative reaction to it.**" *(He says "GLC" — the alias this wiki already carries for the Georgia Lottery Corporation. The counsel is not named here; the corpus carries [[wiki/people/joe-kim.md|Joe Kim]] in that role from Sept 10, and **the identification is an inference**.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]]
- **And the distinction he insisted on, which the counterparty kept collapsing:** "**This is what they want to write into their guidance. The guidance is not the law, and… legislation is not the law. So the law has not passed. The legislation we're not there yet. Their guidance is something that's coming from the top.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]]
- **Neither side thought "same location" was workable.** Cashin: "**they're trying to say you got to… be some other location, whether or not it's ten feet away or what.**" Val: "**To me, it sounds absolutely funny.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]]

**The market, stated to an outsider as three numbers:** "**Seventy four hundred locations, forty seven thousand machines**" and "**Five Billion dollars**" — the same figures as the Sept-4 internal re-measurement and the 47,000 Val gave [[wiki/people/david-sloan.md|David Sloan]] on Sept 9, now given together. Cashin had guessed 6,800 and answered "**Five billion. Didn't know that. B with the B.**" Economics on [[wiki/topics/georgia-prize-card-processor-economics.md]] (Sept 11) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]].

**And a new class of entrant is arriving off the show.** A payment facilitator with its own ATM driving and issuing, which had bolted a generic magstripe card into an off-the-shelf **Gen Mega** ATM six to eight weeks before the August show and is now hunting a sponsor bank. Full profile on [[wiki/organizations/digital-cash-processing.md]]; Val's position on what he will trade for market access is [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-market-access-traded-for-a-share-of-the-back-end-and-the-atm-fee-belongs-to-the-master-operator.md]].

## Open contradictions

**The date of the GACS convenience-store show.** Two sources a week apart give different dates for what appears to be the same show; neither mentions the other and nothing reconciles them. Recorded, not resolved.
- **October 22** — Aug 31 2026, English, a colleague reading the listing aloud to Val in the Georgia office: "There's another show on October twenty-second, which is called the **GACS Southern Convenience Store Petroleum Show**," with Val repeating "That's also October twenty second." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_C_Store_Trade_Show_Go_To_Market-August-31-2026.md]]
- **October 21** — Sept 7 2026, Russian, Andrei Klimovich planning the stand: "У нас следующая выставка[ —] октябрь 21," repeated as the deadline three times ("к октябрю 21… начиная с условного октября 21"). The transcript never names the show; the derived notes identify it as GACS. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]
- **October 21 (second instance)** — Sept 10 2026, Russian, [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]] to the Minsk transactional group, explaining why the MVP-2 target may move: "1 ноября может очень легко стать 20[-]первым октября, то есть там выставка следующая в… [Д]жорджии." He does not name the show either. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-10-requirements-come-first-and-the-recycler-is-out-of-both-mvps.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Capability_Requirements_Workflow_and_MVP_Split-September-10-2026.md]]
- *(A multi-day show spanning both dates would explain it, but no source says so — recorded here as an inference, not a resolution. The count is now two Russian-side sources for the 21st against one English-side reading of the listing for the 22nd; neither side names the show and the listing is the only source that does. **Still not resolved** — a listing read aloud is not obviously weaker evidence than a planner's repeated working date.)*

**The Georgia daily-hold figure (as of Aug 21 2026).** Two irreconcilable figures for the same period were stated in the same session; recorded, not resolved.
- **Val: about $230–240 per day net win**, and earlier that evening "we know for sure that it's making north of $200 per day." Juan corroborated that Val "said, 200." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Machine_Sales_Strategy_Post_GOAM-August-21-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
- **Felipe: $146–147 per day**, from the report he pulled that day, having told Val **$167** in chat earlier: "I told you 100 and sixty-seven. That's that was the last. Today I pulled up the report, 147." He measured "from installation date" onwards, not day to day, and said "I cannot back up the 240 net when you say we have average. I can say it, I can repeat it like a parrot, but… they talk." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Machine_Sales_Strategy_Post_GOAM-August-21-2026.md]]
- **Val's methodological objection (not a reconciliation):** "that's not how you look at the number. You look at the total money in, and you take a percentage of it, because that number shifts all the time… You don't look at net wind from day-to-day." Left unresolved at the end of the meeting. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Machine_Sales_Strategy_Post_GOAM-August-21-2026.md]]

**A third figure, from outside JVL (Sept 12 2026): the Georgia average is "roughly a hundred dollars" a day.** On a phone call about Texas ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-12-halcyon-amusements-texas-net-win-and-lobbying.md|Sept-12]]) Val used Georgia as the comparison baseline — "In Georgia, it's **roughly a hundred dollars**" per device per day — and [[wiki/people/yash-patel.md|Yash Patel]], an operator running machines in both states, answered "**One hundred fourteen**, something like that." **Note the basis is not stated in the source:** the Aug-21 figures above are JVL's own installed Georgia machines, while this exchange reads as a market-wide average across an operator's mixed fleet. Recorded alongside, not merged. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Halcyon_Amusements_Texas_Net_Win_and_Industry_Lobbying-September-12-2026.md]]

**How many machines Felipe has sold in Georgia (as of Aug 21 2026).**

- **Felipe: "I've sold over 3,400 machines in Georgia."** Val: "you have not sold 3,400 machines in Georgia because the bulk of it I sold to Lucky Bucks," arguing that "when I started selling to Lucky Box, that started the movement and everybody who was competing with Lucky Box needed to fill their locations with these machines." Felipe revised to "Okay, so 3200 machines" and then rejected the attribution: "Maybe you think all sales came because of lucky bucks. That might be one way to see. I don't see it that way." Val: "I'm not diminishing the fact that you worked and… that you had an input in the market." Unresolved. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Machine_Sales_Strategy_Post_GOAM-August-21-2026.md]]

### Felipe's "Sales Channel Report" — the point-in-time wave snapshot

Felipe screen-shared his prospect pipeline organised into monthly "waves," each company carrying contact details and a **# of Machines** column defaulting to **2** (the "two-per-customer" starting position Val had set). Wave 3 = September (~24 companies), Wave 4 = October (~29), Wave 5 = November (~21). Val's reaction to the November sheet: "these people, if you went to see them, would they meet with you?" — Felipe: "Most of them would." The company lists themselves sit in the source note; the live pipeline is maintained in the Sales Funnel initiative, outside this wiki. The screenshots referenced by the note are images and out of scope for v1. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Georgia_Machine_Sales_Strategy_Post_GOAM-August-21-2026.md]]

- Named individually during the pipeline walk-through: **Legacy Amusement** ("Legacy definitely wants something for a location coming up. It's a smaller master"), **Everest Amusement** ("for sure is cold"), **VGL** ("I believe we don't need to do trial"), **Fun Time** (already on trial; discussing an advertising deal), **One GA Coin**, **Gold Coin**, **Dixie**, **Time & Amusement**, **Lucky Bucks**, **Diamond Amusement**, and an operator pair ("Riz Juan and his partner") who Val said "want to become a sub-distributor… They probably want a slight, slight discount." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Machine_Sales_Strategy_Post_GOAM-August-21-2026.md]]

## Sept 14 2026 — the Georgia COAM vertical games become the reusable base for another market

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-14-texas-vertical-conversion-and-georgia-game-base.md|Sept-14 6:38 AM ET engineering session]] (Russian) the Georgia COAM game set was chosen as the **donor** for a vertical Texas product, after the attempt to convert JVL's horizontal Texas games to vertical failed on the art. The ruling is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-vertical-texas-starts-from-the-georgia-coam-vertical-games.md]]; what it adds to the Georgia record is production detail about the games themselves. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Texas_Vertical_Conversion_and_Georgia_Game_Base-September-14-2026.md]]

- **Every Georgia game carries a symbol-driven progressive, and the symbol frames are baked into the background video** — [[wiki/people/igor-metelski.md|Igor Metelski]]: "у нас там во всех играх символ прогрессивы. И рамки под эти символ прогресс[ив] запекли [в] фоновое видео." Reusing a Georgia game elsewhere therefore always requires work on the background video. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Vertical_Conversion_and_Georgia_Game_Base-September-14-2026.md]]
- **The designers still hold the un-baked source scenes**, so they can switch off the visibility of those progressive objects and re-bake a clean background video — "запечь на новое видео, где их не будет… вполне себе рабочий вариант." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Vertical_Conversion_and_Georgia_Game_Base-September-14-2026.md]]
- **The last Georgia COAM was built by Igor Metelski and [[wiki/people/anton-leshkevich.md|Anton Leshkevich]]** ("Мы вдвоём с Антоном"), and its **match-3 bonus** is cited by Igor as an example of how much the RAGE bonus library now covers — "даже если мы разрабатываем… относительно новый бонус, как… это мач 3 бонус… для г[а] к[оа]м последнего… нам было на что опереться." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Vertical_Conversion_and_Georgia_Game_Base-September-14-2026.md]]
- **Georgia is also Igor's example of why RAGE is still the faster platform:** for Georgia "мы… эти игры обновляли… делали новый арт", so the maths analysis and scene construction were already done. See [[wiki/topics/nova-platform-architecture.md]] (Sept 14). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Vertical_Conversion_and_Georgia_Game_Base-September-14-2026.md]]
- **Unresolved in the source:** Pavel's "мы выпиливали хенс… тогда… из Джорджия" against Irina's "Но мы его скрыли" — something was either cut out of, or merely hidden in, the Georgia games; the word is garbled and is not interpreted. And the Georgia COAM game whose background video Igor opened on screen is rendered "куриного флюксора" and is not identifiable. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Vertical_Conversion_and_Georgia_Game_Base-September-14-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — game-production content only. **§15.9:** no identifiers in the source.

## Sept 15 2026 — another operator visit: the V3 pitched by its game content for once, and the market explained back to JVL

An in-person visit at the Georgia office, 2:24–3:35 PM ET, with [[wiki/organizations/unlimited-amusement.md|Unlimited Amusement]] — an existing JVL customer ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-15-unlimited-amusement-mega-hit-blaze-v3-and-bundle-pitch.md]]). Teams recorded nothing; the record exists only because Val recorded it on Plaud. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Unlimited_Amusement_Mega_Hit_Blaze_V3_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pitch-September-15-2026.md]]

**What is new for the Georgia record is the game pitch itself.** Across the Sept 1–10 visits the machine is sold on price and on the transactional bundle; here [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]] sells it on **content**, and it is the fullest customer-facing description of **Mega Hit Blaze V3** in the corpus. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Unlimited_Amusement_Mega_Hit_Blaze_V3_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pitch-September-15-2026.md]]

- **Its lineage is named:** "we have studied what our customers want **based on our experience with Peach One, Peach Two, and all these games**, and we released **our new V three called a Mega Hit Blaze game**."
- **Fifteen games, three progressives, all cabinet mechanics.** "**Different types of progressives**… lines games, ways games, everything that people already like and love… **We will have three progressives for all kinds of players. One is more aggressive than the other.** And we've made it **tailored for the [*Kuna*] market** to make sure that it makes money. **It will have 15 games total.**" *(The market word is rendered "Kuna" and is **not** interpreted; COAM is the obvious reading but the source does not say it. The derived notes' glossary separately records one `Coim`→COAM fix elsewhere in the file.)*
- **It is not released yet, and the reason is the count.** "we have **some in testing, not with the full fifteen, but with the previous version**, it is **making great money. It's stable. People like it. People continue ordering, but we don't want to release it just yet until we have the full fifteen.**" That is consistent with the V3 release work tracked above; **no date is given to the customer.**
- **The line-count spread, as spoken:** "**Fifty lines, games of fifty lines**… we've had games of fifty lines before… **All kinds of twenty-five lines**", "we have **high volatility games for those high rollers**. We have **fun bonuses**, and we have new games such as **jackpot. Class three, very easy to play, easy to understand. The more you bet, the higher your jackpot is.**" The design reason given: "**Someone who's betting high will be rewarded accordingly, and they won't be upset because the guy next door betting twenty-five cents just got a jackpot for the same amount that he was betting.**" See [[wiki/topics/slot-game-design-principles.md]].
- **The topper is recommended where ceiling height allows**, with a figure attached — prices on [[wiki/topics/jvl-machine-pricing-strategy.md]].

**What the operator gave back is the better half of the meeting** — an operator's own map of who plays in metro Atlanta, by community and by area; the card-fee complaints that drive player behaviour; the "cannot replay after cashing out" problem they call their biggest; and an unapproved credit-transfer "black box" they have already paid for. All of it is on [[wiki/organizations/unlimited-amusement.md]].

**Two things that bear on the wider Georgia picture:** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Unlimited_Amusement_Mega_Hit_Blaze_V3_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pitch-September-15-2026.md]]

- **Val used the regulator's and the processor's uncertainty as a selling argument, to a customer.** "I met with **Georgia Lottery Corporation**. They are not clear on how everything will function. The lawyer for the association, **[[wiki/people/joe-kim.md|Joe Kim]]**… He is not sure how everything will function. **InComm, the card provider, not sure which card they're gonna end up with**" — therefore buy from a supplier that "will **stay here locally**, will have the **ear to the ground**" and "**grow with you**." This is the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-10-georgia-lottery-coam-manufacturer-introduction.md|Sept-10 regulator meeting]] being used as sales material five days later.
- **His prediction for where the Georgia market settles on service fees:** "**I believe business in Georgia will end up where location does not pay for anything**" — against the operator's "**here mostly all they charge to locations**." Stated as opinion, not as a finding.

**Outcome: one pilot location, no volume, no date — and a new ask, a month-by-month rollout calendar so inventory can be reserved** — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-unlimited-amusement-pilots-one-location.md]]. **No exclusivity was asked for in this meeting**, unlike the Sept-9 visits; recorded as an absence.

**§15.8:** clear — operator, machine and market economics only; no named-person compensation. **§15.9:** no identifiers in either source.

## Sept 15 2026 (evening) — the market's own origin story, told by someone who was at the 2014 launch

From the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-15-billy-liu-playpay-nda-and-georgia-float-economics.md|Sept-15 evening session]] with [[wiki/people/billy-liu.md|Billy Liu]]. **This is the earliest first-hand account of the Georgia COAM connection era in the corpus** — the market as it looked at switch-on. Company detail on [[wiki/organizations/surfnote.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Georgia_Payment_Ecosystem_and_Float_Economics-September-15-2026.md]]

- **The market started at ~18,000 connected machines, in one week.** "**In the very beginning, there was only twenty thousand. Not even. Eighteen thousand connected in the very first week of connections… Georgia started with eighteen thousand machines.**" Against the **47,000 machines / 7,400 locations** the corpus carries for 2026, that is roughly a 2.6× expansion over twelve years *(the comparison is this page's arithmetic, not anything said in the source)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Georgia_Payment_Ecosystem_and_Float_Economics-September-15-2026.md]].
- **Intralot won the 2014 central-monitoring bid, and its board did not work.** "**Back in twenty fourteen, [Intralot] won the bid to become the central monitoring system**… **[Intralot]'s piece of shit didn't work, so we kind of took their glory away. But we also worked with [Intralot] because they are the… partner.**" [[wiki/organizations/surfnote.md|SurfNote]] boards went into about half the initial estate. The Lottery's director at the time is named as **Debbie Alford** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Georgia_Payment_Ecosystem_and_Float_Economics-September-15-2026.md]].
- **SAS connectivity was preceded by a year of paper.** Billy, in the first segment: "**SAS required first of twenty sixteen. They started regulating on a common basis, based on their own readings and pencil and paper. Write down your meter readings. They started on partner systems a year before the actual SAS connectivity.**" *(Speaker labels are **inverted between the two segments** — in this one Billy is Speaker 3 and Val is Speaker 1. The passage is fragmentary and the date is his recollection, not a document's.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Mutual_NDA_Renegotiation-September-15-2026.md]]
- **Before the connection era, one operator held most of the state.** Billy, twice: "**Mike Mackey… when he had ninety-five percent of the marketplace**"; his own family "**had over a thousand machines out**" at the time. *(No page exists for Mike Mackey and none is created on this alone.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Mutual_NDA_Renegotiation-September-15-2026.md]]

### What the incumbents charge, and what JVL's giveaway is aimed at

- **~$500 per unit per machine, paid by the master.** Val: "**they still force master to buy and install the unit… whatever that each unit per machine is. Five hundred dollars. Times six machines, that's three thousand dollars. And if they need a few… points of sale, they need to buy it.**" Against JVL: "**the master doesn't have to pay**… **[JVL] is the only company that gives it away.**" *(The transcript renders the company "GDL" — read as JVL from context, an inference.)* See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-free-ports-and-pos-with-every-machine-bought.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Georgia_Payment_Ecosystem_and_Float_Economics-September-15-2026.md]].
- **Connected-location counts for the incumbents are given and immediately contradicted** — Axis "thirty-four thousand locations", EasyLink "closing on a thousand", then "those two together, you got five thousand". **Nothing is asserted**; see [[wiki/organizations/surfnote.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Georgia_Payment_Ecosystem_and_Float_Economics-September-15-2026.md]].

### Installation cost is the next lever, and it turns on Wi-Fi

Told under the NDA and flagged "**strictly confidential and not to discuss with anybody**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Georgia_Payment_Ecosystem_and_Float_Economics-September-15-2026.md]]:

> "**Right now, we need to run the WSS protocol through wires… The protocol between our devices is WSS protocol, which means you have to lay wires. But the next generation that's coming out now is all Wi-Fi.**"

- **The saving he is chasing:** "**you probably need three hours, two people, three hours for location for wires and the connector. You could shrink it to an hour.**" Even partial success pays: "**if it works sixty percent of the time. That's a big saving.**"
- **And the risk he names himself:** "**I cannot answer the question of how reliable Wi-Fi will be on all of these devices… However, inside a metal cabinet. Because our antenna is on the outside.**" Val's own caveat back: "**what about the reliability? That has to be part of the scaling equation.**"

### And the state of sales, in his own words

"**I just started after the show. I don't have even one installation. Not even one. And maybe I will fail.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Georgia_Payment_Ecosystem_and_Float_Economics-September-15-2026.md]]
